; =========================================================================
; PROJECT 124 DUMP DATA (DOUBLED Z80 INTERFACES)
; =========================================================================

; =========================================================================
; FILE: shared/neogs_io.inc
; =========================================================================
; =========================================================================
; NeoGS ports.inc v0.3 — Project 124 used subset
; Hardware port names/addresses from NedoPC ports.inc v0.3.
; =========================================================================

; ZX Spectrum side
GSCOM           EQU #BB             ; write command / read status
GSSTAT          EQU #BB             ; read CBIT (bit 0), DBIT (bit 7)
GSDAT           EQU #B3             ; read/write data byte
GSCTR           EQU #33             ; global control

B_CBIT          EQU 0
M_CBIT          EQU #01
B_DBIT          EQU 7
M_DBIT          EQU #80

C_GRST          EQU #80
C_GNMI          EQU #40
C_GLED          EQU #20

; NeoGS Z80 local ports
MPAG            EQU #00
MPAGEX          EQU #10             ; independent 16 KiB page for C000-FFFF
ZXCMD           EQU #01
ZXDATRD         EQU #02
ZXDATWR         EQU #03
ZXSTAT          EQU #04
CLRCBIT         EQU #05

GSCFG0          EQU #0F
B_NOROM         EQU 0
M_NOROM         EQU #01
B_RAMRO         EQU 1
M_RAMRO         EQU #02
B_8CHANS        EQU 2
M_8CHANS        EQU #04
B_EXPAG         EQU 3
M_EXPAG         EQU #08
B_CKSEL0        EQU 4
M_CKSEL0        EQU #10
B_CKSEL1        EQU 5
M_CKSEL1        EQU #20
C_10MHZ         EQU #30
C_12MHZ         EQU #10
C_20MHZ         EQU #20
C_24MHZ         EQU #00
B_PAN4CH        EQU 6
M_PAN4CH        EQU #40
B_INV7B         EQU 7
M_INV7B         EQU #80

; NeoGS physical page registers
PG0             EQU #20
PG1             EQU #21
PG2             EQU #22
PG3             EQU #23

; NeoGS DMA registers
DMA_MOD         EQU #1B
DMA_HAD         EQU #1C
DMA_MAD         EQU #1D
DMA_LAD         EQU #1E
DMA_CST         EQU #1F
C_DMA_NONE      EQU 0
C_DMA_ZX        EQU 1
C_DMA_SD        EQU 2
C_DMA_MP3       EQU 3

; Standard General Sound ROM commands.
GS_CMD_RESET_FLAGS  EQU #00
GS_CMD_GET_TOTAL_RAM EQU #20
GS_CMD_LOAD_MODULE  EQU #30
GS_CMD_RUN_MODULE   EQU #31
GS_CMD_OPEN_STREAM  EQU #D1
GS_CMD_CLOSE_STREAM EQU #D2
GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART EQU #F4
GS_CMD_JUMP_ADDRESS EQU #13
GS_CMD_RECEIVE_BLOCK EQU #14

; Project 124 private commands carried by GSCOM after custom firmware starts.
CMD_RESET       EQU #01
CMD_LOAD_X86    EQU #02
CMD_RUN_X86     EQU #03
CMD_GET_STATUS  EQU #04
CMD_MEM_TEST    EQU #05
CMD_BIOS_POST   EQU #07
CMD_INT_TEST    EQU #08
CMD_INT_GUEST_TEST EQU #09
CMD_BIOS_VIDEO_TEST EQU #0A
CMD_RUN_X86_REAL EQU #0B

; Project 124 startup handshake bytes.
NGS_BOOTSTRAP_READY_ACK EQU #A1
NGS_BOOTSTRAP_LENGTH_ACK EQU #A3
NGS_FIRMWARE_READY_ACK  EQU #A2
NGS_BOOTSTRAP_ERROR_ACK EQU #E1
NGS_RESULT_SIGNATURE   EQU #A5

NGS_STATUS_CBIT EQU M_CBIT
NGS_STATUS_DBIT EQU M_DBIT

; The proven x86 DMA frame occupies logical $5000..$7FFF (12 KiB).
; Default NeoGS WIN1 page #03 maps logical $5000 to physical $00D000.
; The two-byte length header leaves #2FFE bytes for the guest image.
NGS_X86_DMA_WINDOW_SIZE EQU #3000
NGS_X86_MAX_CODE_SIZE   EQU #2FFE


; =========================================================================
; FILE: zx_main/main.asm
; =========================================================================
; =========================================================================
; PROJECT 124
; ROOT MODULAR LINKER — DUAL PROCESSOR BUILD
; LINEAR AUTORUN SEQUENCE — FIXED SECTOR LENGTH TRD EXPORT
; =========================================================================

ENABLE_DEBUG_CONSOLE_TEST EQU 0
ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG     EQU 1
ENABLE_TRDOS_DEBUG        EQU 1

        ; --- ВСТРОЕННЫЙ LUA-СКРИПТ АВТОМАТИЧЕСКОГО ДАМПА ПРОЕКТА 124 ---
        LUA
            local out_file = io.open("dump_project_124.txt", "w")
            if out_file then
                out_file:write("; =========================================================================\n")
                out_file:write("; PROJECT 124 DUMP DATA (DOUBLED Z80 INTERFACES)\n")
                out_file:write("; =========================================================================\n\n")

                local files = {
                    "shared/neogs_io.inc",
                    "zx_main/main.asm",
                    "zx_main/main_data.asm",
                    "zx_main/boot_loader.asm",
                    "zx_main/neogs_driver.asm",
                    "zx_main/x86_loader.asm",
                    "zx_main/debug_system.asm",
                    "zx_main/driver_cmos.asm",
                    "zx_main/log_debug.asm",
                    "zx_main/display.asm",
                    "zx_main/bios_console.asm",
                    "zx_main/ui_engine.asm",
                    "zx_main/ui_panels.asm",
                    "zx_main/file_utils.asm",
                    "neogs_sub/main.asm",
                    "neogs_sub/bootstrap.asm",
                    "neogs_sub/x86_interpreter.asm",
                    "neogs_sub/x86_memory.asm",
                    "neogs_sub/x86_modrm.asm",
                    "neogs_sub/x86_interrupt.asm",
                    "neogs_sub/x86_real_mode.asm",
                    "neogs_sub/x86_real_alu.asm",
                    "neogs_sub/x86_real_alu8.asm",
                    "neogs_sub/x86_real_mov.asm",
                    "neogs_sub/bios/bios_core.asm",
                    "neogs_sub/bios/bios_video.asm",
                    "neogs_sub/build_firmware.asm"
                }

                for _, fname in ipairs(files) do
                    out_file:write("; =========================================================================\n")
                    out_file:write("; FILE: " .. fname .. "\n")
                    out_file:write("; =========================================================================\n")
                    local in_file = io.open(fname, "r")
                    if in_file then
                        out_file:write(in_file:read("*all"))
                        in_file:close()
                    else
                        out_file:write("; [LINK ERROR: File " .. fname .. " not found]\n")
                    end
                    out_file:write("\n\n")
                end
                out_file:close()
            end
        ENDLUA

        DEVICE ZXSPECTRUM128
        EMPTYTRD "disk_A.trd"

        ; ГЛОБАЛЬНАЯ КОНСТАНТА БЕЗОПАСНОГО БУФЕРА КАТАЛОГА / ДАННЫХ
TRDOS_PANEL_BUFFER EQU #C000

        ; Базовый адрес старта основного кода Spectrum — 24576 ($6000)
        ORG 24576

; Первичный прыжок на старт, прецизионно выровненный на адрес 24585
        jp      Init_Z80_System
        BLOCK   6, 0            

; Сюда прыгает бут-загрузчик из строки 30 по команде RANDOMIZE USR VAL "24585"
Init_Z80_System::
        di

        ; АТОМАРНЫЙ МАРКЕР СТАРТА: Выстреливаем байт #AA (170) напрямую в порт #EF
        ld      a, #AA
        out     (#EF), a

        ld      (OLD_SP), sp    
        ld      sp, $AFFF       ; Переносим стек в безопасную зону проекта 124

        ld      a, #10
        ld      bc, #7FFD
        out     (C), a
        ld      (#5C3B), a

        xor     a
        ld      bc, #EFF7
        out     (C), a          ; Инициализация Pentagon-расширения

        ; Raw startup marker: confirms Init_Z80_System reached its end.
        ld      a, 'I'
        out     (#EF), a
        jp      START_APPLICATION

; Подключение системы отладки и макросов порта #EF
        INCLUDE "debug_system.asm"

START_APPLICATION:
        ; Raw markers bypass timestamp/RTC code so an early hang is visible.
        ld      a, 'C'
        out     (#EF), a

        ; ЭТАП 1 — ЗАПУСК CMOS ЧАСОВ К512ВИ1
        call    CHECK_AND_INIT_CMOS
        ld      a, 'D'
        out     (#EF), a
        ld      a, 'E'
        out     (#EF), a
        MACRO_EF_STRING msg_stage1

        ; ЭТАП 2 — ДВУХСТУПЕНЧАТАЯ ЗАГРУЗКА ПРОШИВКИ NEOGS
        ; GS105a #14 загружает исполняемый bootstrap в RAM $4000;
        ; команда #13 запускает его, после чего он включает RAM-over-ROM,
        ; принимает образ в адрес $0000 и передаёт управление новой прошивке.
        ; One TR-DOS package contains bootstrap + firmware; Stage 2 reads it once.
        MACRO_EF_STRING msg_stage2_file_start
        call    load_neogs_firmware_file
        jr      nc, .firmware_file_loaded
        MACRO_EF_STRING msg_stage2_file_fail
        ld      a, (neo_fw_file_error)
        ld      l, a
        ld      h, 0
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 HL
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
        di
.firmware_file_halt:
        halt
        jr      .firmware_file_halt

.firmware_file_loaded:
        MACRO_EF_STRING msg_stage2_file_ok
        MACRO_EF_STRING msg_stage2_file_bytes
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_file_length)
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 HL
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
        MACRO_EF_STRING msg_stage2_file_sectors
        ld      a, (neo_fw_load_remaining)
        inc     a                       ; first sector was already read
        ld      l, a
        ld      h, 0
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 HL
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
        ld      hl, #C000
        ld      de, (neo_fw_file_length)
        call    upload_firmware
        jr      c, .firmware_upload_failed
        MACRO_EF_STRING msg_stage2_stream_ok
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_bytes_sent)
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 HL
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
        MACRO_EF_STRING msg_stage2
        jp      .firmware_upload_done

.firmware_upload_failed:
        MACRO_EF_STRING msg_stage2_fail
        MACRO_EF_STRING msg_stage2_sent
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_bytes_sent)
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 HL
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
        MACRO_EF_STRING msg_stage2_initial
        ld      a, (neo_fw_initial_status)
        ld      l, a
        ld      h, 0
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 HL
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
        MACRO_EF_STRING msg_stage2_status
        ld      a, (neo_fw_last_status)
        ld      l, a
        ld      h, 0
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 HL
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
        MACRO_EF_STRING msg_stage2_phase
        ld      a, (neo_fw_timeout_phase)
        ld      l, a
        ld      h, 0
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 HL
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
        MACRO_EF_STRING msg_stage2_rx
        ld      a, (neo_fw_last_rx_byte)
        ld      l, a
        ld      h, 0
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 HL
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
        MACRO_EF_STRING msg_stage2_bootstrap
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_bootstrap_address)
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 HL
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
        MACRO_EF_STRING msg_stage2_tx_byte
        ld      a, (neo_fw_last_tx_byte)
        ld      l, a
        ld      h, 0
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 HL
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
        MACRO_EF_STRING msg_stage2_tx_state
        ld      a, (neo_fw_tx_wait_state)
        ld      l, a
        ld      h, 0
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 HL
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
        MACRO_EF_STRING msg_stage2_stream_left
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_stream_remaining)
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 HL
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
        MACRO_EF_STRING msg_stage2_stream_src
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_source)
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 HL
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
        MACRO_EF_STRING msg_stage2_buffer_left
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_buffer_remaining)
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 HL
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
        MACRO_EF_STRING msg_stage2_trdos_cursor
        ld      a, (neo_fw_load_track)
        ld      h, a
        ld      a, (neo_fw_load_sector)
        ld      l, a
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 HL
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
        di
.firmware_upload_halt:
        halt
        jr      .firmware_upload_halt

.firmware_upload_done:

        ; ЭТАП 3 — ИНИЦИАЛИЗАЦИЯ И ОТРИСОВКА ИНТЕРФЕЙСА ОКОН И ДВУХ ШРИФТОВ
        call    INIT_PROGRAM_INTERFACE

        MACRO_EF_STRING msg_stage3_ok

        ; ЭТАП 4 — ВЫВОД СТАРТОВОГО ОТЧЕТА В НИЖНЕЕ ОКНО ЛОГОВ COMMANDER
        ld      hl, msg_log_screen_ready
        call    LOG_WriteMessageToBuffer  

        ld      hl, msg_log_sp_prefix
        call    LOG_WriteMessageToBuffer  
        ld      hl, (OLD_SP)
        MACRO_EF_HEX16  hl
        MACRO_EF_CHAR   13
        MACRO_EF_STRING msg_stage4
        call    BIOS_DebugConsole_Init

        ; ЭТАП 5 — АВТОМАТИЧЕСКИЙ ЛИНЕЙНЫЙ ПЕРЕХОД К ЗАГРУЗКЕ И IPC ТРАНСЛЯЦИИ ЗАДАЧИ
        ld      hl, task_filename
        ld      a, 'C'
        
        ; ВАЖНО: не переключаемся на старый TR-DOS SP (#Cxxx).
        ; Буфер чтения начинается с #C000 и перезаписывает эту область.
        ; Stage 5 сам временно переносит рабочий стек на #BFFF, а этот
        ; CALL остаётся на безопасном прикладном стеке (#AFFF).
        di
        call    EXECUTE_X86_FILE_STAGE
        ei
        jr      c, .x86_stage_failed

        ; Финальная точка останова ядра после завершения всех этапов
.core_halt:
        MACRO_EF_STRING msg_stage_all_complete
        di
        halt
        jr      .core_halt

; Ошибка выполнения этапа загрузки/запуска x86-задачи.
.x86_stage_failed:
        MACRO_EF_STRING msg_stage5_fail
        di
.x86_stage_failed_halt:
        halt
        jr      .x86_stage_failed_halt

msg_stage5_fail:
        DEFB "[CORE] ERROR: x86 task pipeline failed; startup halted.", 13, 0

; Ошибка загрузки прошивки NeoGS. Не продолжаем работу с неподтвержденным ядром.
msg_stage2_fail:
        DEFB "[CORE] ERROR: NeoGS ROM bootstrap / firmware handshake failed.", 13, 0
msg_stage2_sent:
        DEFB "[FW] DATA BYTES SENT = ", 0
msg_stage2_initial:
        DEFB "[FW] INITIAL GSSTAT #BB = ", 0
msg_stage2_status:
        DEFB "[FW] LAST GSSTAT #BB = ", 0
msg_stage2_phase:
        DEFB "[FW] BOOT PHASE (1=#F4,2=#20,3=#14,4=DATA,5=#13,6=BOOT,7=LEN,8=FW,9=READY,10=COUNT) = ", 0
msg_stage2_rx:
        DEFB "[FW] LAST RX BYTE = ", 0
msg_stage2_bootstrap:
        DEFB "[FW] BOOTSTRAP ADDRESS = ", 0
msg_stage2_tx_byte:
        DEFB "[FW] LAST TX BYTE = ", 0
msg_stage2_tx_state:
        DEFB "[FW] TX WAIT STATE (0=ACK,1=FREE SLOT,2=CONSUME ACK) = ", 0
msg_stage2_stream_left:
        DEFB "[FW] V2 STREAM BYTES LEFT = ", 0
msg_stage2_stream_src:
        DEFB "[FW] V2 STREAM SOURCE = ", 0
msg_stage2_buffer_left:
        DEFB "[FW] TRDOS BUFFER BYTES LEFT = ", 0
msg_stage2_trdos_cursor:
        DEFB "[FW] NEXT TRDOS TRACK:SECTOR = ", 0
msg_stage2_file_start:
        DEFB "[FW] Loading neoprog.C from TR-DOS into #C000...", 13, 0
msg_stage2_file_ok:
        DEFB "[FW] neoprog.C first sector loaded; remaining sectors stream during upload.", 13, 0
msg_stage2_file_bytes:
        DEFB "[FW] PACKAGE LENGTH (BYTES) = #", 0
msg_stage2_file_sectors:
        DEFB "[FW] PACKAGE SECTORS = #", 0
msg_stage2_stream_ok:
        DEFB "[FW] STREAM ACK; GS DATA BYTES CONFIRMED = #", 0
msg_stage2_file_fail:
        DEFB "[FW] ERROR: neoprog.C missing, corrupt or unreadable; code=#", 0

; =========================================================================
; СТРОГАЯ ПОСЛЕДОВАТЕЛЬНАЯ СБОРКА ИЗОЛИРОВАННЫХ МОДУЛЕЙ ИНТЕРФЕЙСА ХОСТА
; =========================================================================
        INCLUDE "main_data.asm"     
        INCLUDE "driver_cmos.asm"   
        INCLUDE "neogs_driver.asm"  
        INCLUDE "x86_loader.asm"    
        INCLUDE "log_debug.asm"     
        
        INCLUDE "display.asm"       ; Модуль низкоуровневого вывода знакомест
        INCLUDE "ui_engine.asm"     ; Модуль прошивки шрифтов и скроллера
        INCLUDE "bios_console.asm"  ; 42-column 6x8 BIOS debug console
        INCLUDE "ui_panels.asm"     ; Модуль высокоуровневых панелей Commander
        INCLUDE "file_utils.asm"    ; Модуль живой работы с диском TR-DOS

; NeoGS code is excluded from the resident host image proj124.C.
; The package is placed in bank 1 only as the source for the separate TR-DOS file.
ProgramEnd:
        ; Keep at least 2 KiB between the loaded program and the application
        ; stack growing down from #AFFF. NeoGS code is not resident in proj124.C.
        ASSERT ProgramEnd <= #A800

; Package chunk sizes are generated by pack_neogs.ps1 before this assembly.
; The complete package is exported to TR-DOS from multiple 16-KiB banks below.
        INCLUDE "neoprog_chunks.inc"
        INCLUDE "neoprog_v2_chunks.inc"

; Return to bank 0 and place the BASIC boot program at #E000.
        PAGE    0
        ORG     #E000
        INCLUDE "boot_loader.asm"

; NeoGS firmware is built as a separate binary by neogs_sub/build_firmware.asm.

; =========================================================================
; СЕКТОР ФИНАЛЬНОГО ОКОНЧАТЕЛЬНОГО ЭКСПОРТА НА ДИСК
; =========================================================================
        PAGE    0                       ; Возвращаем базовый нулевой банк памяти хоста

        ; Export the host and boot files to the normal V1 disk.
        SAVETRD "disk_A.trd", "proj124.C", 24576, ProgramEnd - 24576
        SAVETRD "disk_A.trd", "boot.B", BASIC_START, BASIC_END - BASIC_START

        ; V1 firmware package (default filename used by the existing host).
        ASSERT NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK_COUNT >= 1
        ASSERT NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK_COUNT <= 4
        ASSERT NEOGS_PACKAGE_TOTAL_SIZE <= #FF00
        PAGE    1
        ORG     #C000
neoprog_chunk0_start:
        INCBIN "zx_main/neoprog_part0.bin"
neoprog_chunk0_end:
        ASSERT neoprog_chunk0_end - neoprog_chunk0_start = NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK0_SIZE
        SAVETRD "disk_A.trd", "neoprog.C", #C000, NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK0_SIZE

        IF NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK_COUNT > 1
        PAGE    3
        ORG     #C000
neoprog_chunk1_start:
        INCBIN "zx_main/neoprog_part1.bin"
neoprog_chunk1_end:
        ASSERT neoprog_chunk1_end - neoprog_chunk1_start = NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK1_SIZE
        SAVETRD "disk_A.trd",&"neoprog.C", #C000, NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK1_SIZE
        ENDIF

        IF NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK_COUNT > 2
        PAGE    4
        ORG     #C000
neoprog_chunk2_start:
        INCBIN "zx_main/neoprog_part2.bin"
neoprog_chunk2_end:
        ASSERT neoprog_chunk2_end - neoprog_chunk2_start = NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK2_SIZE
        SAVETRD "disk_A.trd",&"neoprog.C", #C000, NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK2_SIZE
        ENDIF

        IF NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK_COUNT > 3
        PAGE    6
        ORG     #C000
neoprog_chunk3_start:
        INCBIN "zx_main/neoprog_part3.bin"
neoprog_chunk3_end:
        ASSERT neoprog_chunk3_end - neoprog_chunk3_start = NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK3_SIZE
        SAVETRD "disk_A.trd",&"neoprog.C", #C000, NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK3_SIZE
        ENDIF

        ; V2 staged package is also stored on the normal disk under a distinct
        ; filename. The post-build disk copy makes it the active neoprog.C.
        ASSERT NEOGS_V2_PACKAGE_CHUNK_COUNT >= 1
        ASSERT NEOGS_V2_PACKAGE_CHUNK_COUNT <= 4
        ASSERT NEOGS_V2_PACKAGE_TOTAL_SIZE <= #FF00
        PAGE    1
        ORG     #C000
neoprog_v2_chunk0_start:
        INCBIN "zx_main/neoprog_v2_part0.bin"
neoprog_v2_chunk0_end:
        ASSERT neoprog_v2_chunk0_end - neoprog_v2_chunk0_start = NEOGS_V2_PACKAGE_CHUNK0_SIZE
        SAVETRD "disk_A.trd", "neopv2.C", #C000, NEOGS_V2_PACKAGE_CHUNK0_SIZE

        IF NEOGS_V2_PACKAGE_CHUNK_COUNT > 1
        PAGE    3
        ORG     #C000
neoprog_v2_chunk1_start:
        INCBIN "zx_main/neoprog_v2_part1.bin"
neoprog_v2_chunk1_end:
        ASSERT neoprog_v2_chunk1_end - neoprog_v2_chunk1_start = NEOGS_V2_PACKAGE_CHUNK1_SIZE
        SAVETRD "disk_A.trd",&"neopv2.C", #C000, NEOGS_V2_PACKAGE_CHUNK1_SIZE
        ENDIF

        IF NEOGS_V2_PACKAGE_CHUNK_COUNT > 2
        PAGE    4
        ORG     #C000
neoprog_v2_chunk2_start:
        INCBIN "zx_main/neoprog_v2_part2.bin"
neoprog_v2_chunk2_end:
        ASSERT neoprog_v2_chunk2_end - neoprog_v2_chunk2_start = NEOGS_V2_PACKAGE_CHUNK2_SIZE
        SAVETRD "disk_A.trd",&"neopv2.C", #C000, NEOGS_V2_PACKAGE_CHUNK2_SIZE
        ENDIF

        IF NEOGS_V2_PACKAGE_CHUNK_COUNT > 3
        PAGE    6
        ORG     #C000
neoprog_v2_chunk3_start:
        INCBIN "zx_main/neoprog_v2_part3.bin"
neoprog_v2_chunk3_end:
        ASSERT neoprog_v2_chunk3_end - neoprog_v2_chunk3_start = NEOGS_V2_PACKAGE_CHUNK3_SIZE
        SAVETRD "disk_A.trd",&"neopv2.C", #C000, NEOGS_V2_PACKAGE_CHUNK3_SIZE
        ENDIF

        PAGE    0
        SAVETRD "disk_A.trd", "x86task.C", x86_diag_program, x86_diag_program_end - x86_diag_program

; =========================================================================


; =========================================================================
; FILE: zx_main/main_data.asm
; =========================================================================
; =========================================================================
; FILE: zx_main/main_data.asm
; PROJECT 124 — global state and messages
; =========================================================================

Console_X::         DB 0
Console_Y::         DB 0
OLD_SP::            DW 0
APP_SP_BACKUP::     DW 0
Variable_Backup:    DW 0
                    DW 0
                    DW 0
                    DB 0

; Public result copy retained for other application modules.
X86_RESULT_AX::     DW 0

; Port #EF diagnostic messages.
msg_stage1:    DEFB "[CORE] Stage 1: Clock RTC K512VI1 checked and synced successfully.",13,0
msg_stage2:    DEFB "[CORE] Stage 2: Bootstrapping custom NeoGS Z80 firmware through GS105a ROM loader...",13,0
msg_stage3_ok: DEFB "[GUI] Stage 3: Custom fonts linked. Two-window application workspace rendered.",13,0
msg_stage4:    DEFB "[CORE] Stage 4: Execution window stabilized. Bypassing interactive loop.",13,0
msg_stage_all_complete: DEFB "[HALT] Core task pipeline executed entirely. System suspended.",13,0

; Screen log strings.
msg_log_screen_ready: DEFB "[SYS_START] PERFECT MANAGER 124 DUAL-CORE NODE ONLINE.",0
msg_log_sp_prefix:    DEFB "[SYS_INFO] ORIGINAL TR-DOS SYSTEM STACK POINTER WAS SAVED AT: #",0
msg_log_exec_x86:     DEFB "[EXECUTE] AUTOMATIC LINEAR TRANSFER INITIATED...",0

; Eight-character TR-DOS filename, extension supplied separately as 'C'.
task_filename:        DEFB "x86task "


; =========================================================================
; FILE: zx_main/boot_loader.asm
; =========================================================================
; =========================================================================
; FILE: zx_main/boot_loader.asm
; =========================================================================
; PROJECT 124 — TR-DOS BASIC BOOTLOADER (ПОЛНЫЙ БИНАРНЫЙ КОД ЦЕЛИКОМ)
; ОПИСАНИЕ: Исправлен барьер CLEAR для предотвращения порчи системных переменных
; =========================================================================

CLEAR       EQU     $FD
LOAD        EQU     $EF
CODE        EQU     $AF
RANDOMIZE   EQU     $F9
USR         EQU     $C0
REM         EQU     $EA
VAL         EQU     $B0
OUT_CMD     EQU     $DF             ; Токен команды OUT ($DF) из карты ПЗУ

BASIC_START:
basic_loader:
        ; --- Строка 10: OUT 239,1: CLEAR 24000 ---
        ; 🔥 ФИКС: Опускаем границу CLEAR до 24000, защищая всю область памяти с адреса 24576
        DB      0, 10                   
        DW      LINE10_END-LINE10       
LINE10:
        DB      OUT_CMD                 ; OUT
        DB      "239", $0E, $00, $00, $EF, $00, $00 ; Бинарный маркер числа 239
        DB      ","
        DB      "1", $0E, $00, $00, $01, $00, $00   ; Бинарный маркер числа 1
        DB      ":"
        DB      CLEAR                   ; CLEAR
        DB      "24000", $0E, $00, $00, $5D, $C0, $00 ; Бинарный маркер числа 24000 (#5DC0)
        DB      $0D                     
LINE10_END:

        ; --- Строка 11: Вывод приветствия "P124 START" в порт отладки #EF ---
        DB      0, 11
        DW      LINE11_END-LINE11
LINE11:
        DB      OUT_CMD, VAL, 34, "239", 34, ",", VAL, 34, "50", 34, ":"
        DB      OUT_CMD, VAL, 34, "239", 34, ",", VAL, 34, "31", 34, ":"
        DB      OUT_CMD, VAL, 34, "239", 34, ",", VAL, 34, "32", 34, ":"
        DB      OUT_CMD, VAL, 34, "239", 34, ",", VAL, 34, "34", 34, ":"
        DB      OUT_CMD, VAL, 34, "239", 34, ",", VAL, 34, "32", 34, ":"
        DB      OUT_CMD, VAL, 34, "239", 34, ",", VAL, 34, "83", 34, ":"
        DB      OUT_CMD, VAL, 34, "239", 34, ",", VAL, 34, "84", 34, ":"
        DB      OUT_CMD, VAL, 34, "239", 34, ",", VAL, 34, "65", 34, ":"
        DB      OUT_CMD, VAL, 34, "239", 34, ",", VAL, 34, "82", 34, ":"
        DB      OUT_CMD, VAL, 34, "239", 34, ",", VAL, 34, "84", 34, ":"
        DB      OUT_CMD, VAL, 34, "239", 34, ",", VAL, 34, "13", 34, ":"
        DB      OUT_CMD, VAL, 34, "239", 34, ",", VAL, 34, "10", 34
        DB      $0D
LINE11_END:

        ; --- Строка 20: RANDOMIZE USR 15619 : REM : LOAD "proj124" CODE 24576 ---
        DB      0, 20                   
        DW      LINE20_END-LINE20       
LINE20:
        DB      RANDOMIZE               ; RANDOMIZE
        DB      USR, "15619", $0E, 0, 0 ; USR 15619
        DW      15619                   
        DB      0
        DB      $3A, REM, $3A           
        DB      "LOAD ", 34, "proj124", 34, " CODE 24576", $0D 
LINE20_END:

        ; --- Строка 21: Вывод метки "LOADED" в порт отладки #EF после чтения файла ---
        DB      0, 21
        DW      LINE21_END-LINE21
LINE21:
        DB      OUT_CMD, VAL, 34, "239", 34, ",", VAL, 34, "76", 34, ":"
        DB      OUT_CMD, VAL, 34, "239", 34, ",", VAL, 34, "79", 34, ":"
        DB      OUT_CMD, VAL, 34, "239", 34, ",", VAL, 34, "65", 34, ":"
        DB      OUT_CMD, VAL, 34, "239", 34, ",", VAL, 34, "68", 34, ":"
        DB      OUT_CMD, VAL, 34, "239", 34, ",", VAL, 34, "69", 34, ":"
        DB      OUT_CMD, VAL, 34, "239", 34, ",", VAL, 34, "68", 34, ":"
        DB      OUT_CMD, VAL, 34, "239", 34, ",", VAL, 34, "13", 34, ":"
        DB      OUT_CMD, VAL, 34, "239", 34, ",", VAL, 34, "10", 34
        DB      $0D
LINE21_END:

        ; --- Строка 30: OUT 239,2: RANDOMIZE USR VAL "24585" ---
        DB      0, 30                   
        DW      LINE30_END-LINE30       
LINE30:
        DB      OUT_CMD                 ; OUT
        DB      "239", $0E, $00, $00, $EF, $00, $00 ; Бинарный маркер числа 239
        DB      ","
        DB      "2", $0E, $00, $00, $02, $00, $00   ; Бинарный маркер числа 2
        DB      ":"
        DB      RANDOMIZE               ; RANDOMIZE
        DB      USR                     ; USR ($C0)
        DB      VAL                     ; VAL ($B0)
        DB      34                      
        DEFM    "24585"                 ; Переход на Init_Z80_System
        DB      34                      
        DB      $0D                     
LINE30_END:

        DB      $80                     ; Системный маркер конца BASIC-программы

BASIC_END:


; =========================================================================
; FILE: zx_main/neogs_driver.asm
; =========================================================================
; =========================================================================
; PROJECT 124 — NeoGS host-side driver
; Stage 2 uses GS105a #14 (RECEIVE BLOCK) and #13 (JUMP) to start a two-stage bootstrap.
; =========================================================================
        INCLUDE "../shared/neogs_io.inc"

FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT EQU #FFFF
IPC_BYTE_TIMEOUT EQU #FFFF
IPC_CMD_TIMEOUT EQU #FFFF

; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
; Open the single NeoGS package on TR-DOS and read its first sector to #C000.
; The same 256-byte buffer is reused for every following sector while the
; firmware is streamed to NeoGS. The complete file is NEVER staged in host RAM.
; OUT: Carry clear, first sector at #C000, file length and next-sector cursor set.
; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
load_neogs_firmware_file::
        ; Search the exact 8.3 catalogue name neoprog.C.
        ld      hl, neo_fw_filename
        ld      de, #5CDD
        ld      bc, 8
        ldir
        ld      a, 'C'
        ld      (#5CE5), a
        ld      a, 9
        ld      (#5D06), a
        ld      c, 10
        call    #3D13
        ld      a, c
        cp      #FF
        jp      z, .file_not_found

        ; C=#0A returns the directory slot. Fetch its full descriptor with C=#08.
        push    af
        xor     a
        ld      (#5D0F), a
        ld      (#5D10), a
        pop     af
        ld      c, 8
        call    #3D13
        ld      a, (#5D0F)
        ld      b, a
        ld      a, (#5D10)
        or      b
        jp      nz, .bad_descriptor

        ; Store the actual package length; its header separates the two code sections.
        ld      hl, (#5CE8)
        ld      (neo_fw_file_length), hl
        ld      a, h
        or      l
        jp      z, .bad_length
        ld      a, h
        or      a
        jr      nz, .package_minimum_ok
        ld      a, l
        cp      3
        jp      c, .bad_length
.package_minimum_ok:
        ; Verify sector count = ceil(file_length / 256).
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_file_length)
        ld      de, 255
        add     hl, de
        ld      a, (#5CEA)
        cp      h
        jp      nz, .bad_descriptor
        or      a
        jp      z, .bad_descriptor
        ld      (neo_fw_load_remaining), a

        ld      a, (#5CEB)
        cp      16
        jp      nc, .bad_descriptor
        ld      (neo_fw_load_sector), a
        ld      a, (#5CEC)
        ld      (neo_fw_load_track), a
        ld      hl, #C000
        ld      (neo_fw_load_buffer), hl

        ; Read only the first sector now. Later sectors are fetched on demand
        ; by upload_firmware after the preceding buffer bytes have been sent.
        call    neo_fw_read_next_sector
        jp      c, .sector_read_failed
        xor     a
        ret

.file_not_found:
        ld      a, 1
        jr      .load_failed
.bad_descriptor:
        ld      a, 2
        jr      .load_failed
.bad_length:
        ld      a, 3
        jr      .load_failed
.sector_read_failed:
        ld      a, 4
.load_failed:
        ld      (neo_fw_file_error), a
        scf
        ret

; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
; Read one TR-DOS sector into the reusable #C000 buffer.
; Uses neo_fw_load_track/sector/remaining. Advances the cursor only after a
; successful read. OUT: carry clear; carry set on disk error/exhaustion.
; Clobbers AF, BC, DE, HL.
neo_fw_read_next_sector:
        ld      a, (neo_fw_load_remaining)
        or      a
        jr      nz, .sector_available
        scf
        ret
.sector_available:
        ld      a, (neo_fw_load_track)
        ld      d, a
        ld      a, (neo_fw_load_sector)
        ld      e, a
        ld      hl, #C000
        ld      b, 1
        ld      c, 5
        xor     a
        ld      (#5D0F), a
        ld      (#5D10), a
        call    #3D13
        ld      a, (#5D0F)
        ld      b, a
        ld      a, (#5D10)
        or      b
        jr      nz, .sector_io_error

        ; Advance TR-DOS cursor: sectors 0..15, then next track.
        ld      a, (neo_fw_load_sector)
        inc     a
        cp      16
        jr      c, .sector_same_track
        xor     a
        ld      (neo_fw_load_sector), a
        ld      a, (neo_fw_load_track)
        inc     a
        ld      (neo_fw_load_track), a
        jr      .sector_cursor_advanced
.sector_same_track:
        ld      (neo_fw_load_sector), a
.sector_cursor_advanced:
        ld      a, (neo_fw_load_remaining)
        dec     a
        ld      (neo_fw_load_remaining), a
        or      a
        ret
.sector_io_error:
        scf
        ret

; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
; upload_firmware
; IN : HL = #C000 reusable first-sector buffer, DE = total package length.
; OUT: Carry clear only after custom firmware sends NGS_FIRMWARE_READY_ACK.
;
; Cold/warm reset path:
;   #F4 cold restart
;   #20 get total RAM, read three bytes to prove stock ROM is ready
;   #14 receive executable bootstrap directly into RAM at $4000
;   #13 jump to $4000
;   #A1 bootstrap ready, send length, receive #A3 length accepted
;   send custom firmware bytes to RAM $0000, receive #A2 firmware ready
; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
upload_firmware::
        ; Only the first sector is resident at #C000. Remaining sectors are
        ; read into this same buffer as the firmware stream consumes them.
        ; Layout: WORD bootstrap_length, bootstrap bytes, then firmware bytes.
        ld      (neo_fw_package_source), hl
        ld      (neo_fw_package_length), de

        ; Decode and validate the bootstrap length from the package header.
        ld      a, d
        or      a
        jr      nz, .package_header_present
        ld      a, e
        cp      3
        jp      c, .upload_failed
.package_header_present:
        ld      a, (hl)
        ld      (neo_fw_bootstrap_length), a
        inc     hl
        ld      a, (hl)
        ld      (neo_fw_bootstrap_length+1), a
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
        ld      a, h
        or      l
        jp      z, .upload_failed
        ; Bootstrap must fit entirely in the first 256-byte sector because it
        ; is needed before the receiver can be started. The package itself may
        ; be much larger; only this first sector is buffered at a time.
        ld      a, h
        or      a
        jp      nz, .upload_failed
        ld      a, l
        cp      255                   ; maximum length 254 bytes
        jp      nc, .upload_failed

        ; V2 has an explicit V2S1 marker after its stage-0 image. Check only
        ; when the marker and stage-1 length word are guaranteed to be in
        ; sector 0 (stage0 <= 248). A non-match follows the untouched v1 path.
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
        ld      a, h
        or      a
        jr      nz, .v1_package_format
        ld      a, l
        cp      249
        jr      nc, .v1_package_format
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_package_source)
        inc     hl
        inc     hl
        ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
        add     hl, de
        ld      a, (hl)
        cp      'V'
        jr      nz, .v1_package_format
        inc     hl
        ld      a, (hl)
        cp      '2'
        jr      nz, .v1_package_format
        inc     hl
        ld      a, (hl)
        cp      'S'
        jr      nz, .v1_package_format
        inc     hl
        ld      a, (hl)
        cp      '1'
        jp      z, upload_firmware_v2
.v1_package_format:

        ; Firmware length = package length - header - bootstrap length.
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_package_length)
        ld      de, 2
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      c, .upload_failed
        ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      c, .upload_failed
        ld      (neo_fw_length), hl

        ; Bootstrap and the beginning of firmware follow the two-byte header
        ; in the first sector. The firmware pointer is advanced as bytes go.
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_package_source)
        inc     hl
        inc     hl
        ld      (neo_fw_bootstrap_source), hl
        ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
        add     hl, de
        ld      (neo_fw_source), hl

        ; Number of firmware bytes remaining in the first sector:
        ; 256 - (2-byte header + bootstrap length) = 254 - bootstrap length.
        ld      hl, 254
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        ld      (neo_fw_buffer_remaining), hl

        ; V1 resident firmware is still limited to WIN0. During transfer the bootstrap
        ; stack remains at #FFFE in WIN3; the resident entry later moves its stack
        ; to WIN1/#4FFE, leaving WIN2 available for the future banked-image format.
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_length)
        ld      a, h
        or      a
        jp      nz, .firmware_high_nonzero
        ld      a, l
        or      a
        jp      z, .upload_failed
        jr      .firmware_length_valid
.firmware_high_nonzero:
        ld      a, h
        cp      #40
        jr      c, .firmware_length_valid
        jp      nz, .upload_failed
        ld      a, l
        or      a
        jp      nz, .upload_failed
.firmware_length_valid:
        xor     a
        ld      (neo_fw_bytes_sent), a
        ld      (neo_fw_bytes_sent+1), a
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        in      a, (GSSTAT)
        ld      (neo_fw_initial_status), a
        ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a

        ; Phase 1: documented GS105a cold restart. Also returns a running
        ; Project 124 firmware to the stock ROM before the new bootstrap.
        ld      a, 1
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        ld      a, GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART
        call    gs_boot_send_command_long
        jp      c, .upload_failed

        ; Phase 2: #20 returns three RAM-size bytes only after stock ROM is up.
        ; Retry #F4 once: if the previous run was interrupted during DMA, the
        ; first #F4 may have been consumed as the DMA end marker; the second
        ; reaches the command dispatcher after it returns to its main loop.
        ld      a, 2
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        call    gs_boot_probe_rom
        jr      nc, .stock_rom_ready
        ld      a, 1
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        ld      a, GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART
        call    gs_boot_send_command_long
        jp      c, .upload_failed
        ld      a, 2
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        call    gs_boot_probe_rom
        jp      c, .upload_failed
.stock_rom_ready:

        ; Phase 3: use the native GS105a memory loader, not the MOD player.
        ; #14 RECEIVE BLOCK expects the first (negative length low) byte
        ; before the command, then negative length high, address low/high,
        ; and exactly N payload bytes. The payload is written directly to RAM.
        ld      a, 3
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        ld      hl, 0
        ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de                  ; HL = -bootstrap length
        ld      (neo_fw_block_length), hl
        ld      a, l
        call    gs_boot_write_command_argument
        jp      c, .upload_failed
        ld      a, GS_CMD_RECEIVE_BLOCK
        call    gs_boot_send_command
        jp      c, .upload_failed

        ; Remaining #14 header: -length high, destination $4000.
        ld      a, (neo_fw_block_length+1)
        call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
        jp      c, .upload_failed
        xor     a
        call    gs_boot_send_data_byte  ; destination low = $00
        jp      c, .upload_failed
        ld      a, #40
        call    gs_boot_send_data_byte  ; destination high = $40
        jp      c, .upload_failed

        ; Phase 4: transfer the executable bootstrap into RAM $4000.
        ld      a, 4
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_bootstrap_source)
        ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
.bootstrap_block_loop:
        ld      a, d
        or      e
        jr      z, .bootstrap_block_done
        ld      a, (hl)
        call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
        jp      c, .upload_failed
        inc     hl
        dec     de
        jr      .bootstrap_block_loop
.bootstrap_block_done:

        ; Phase 5: #13 JUMP TO ADDRESS. The low byte is the command's
        ; first argument and must be written before issuing the command.
        ld      a, 5
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        xor     a                       ; entry address low = $00
        call    gs_boot_write_command_argument
        jp      c, .upload_failed
        ld      a, GS_CMD_JUMP_ADDRESS
        call    gs_boot_send_command
        jp      c, .upload_failed
        ld      a, #40                  ; entry address high = $40
        call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
        jp      c, .upload_failed

        ; Phase 6: bootstrap has enabled RAM-over-ROM and is waiting at $0000.
        ld      a, 6
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
        jp      c, .upload_failed
        cp      NGS_BOOTSTRAP_READY_ACK
        jp      nz, .upload_failed

        ; Phase 7: send firmware length low/high and require ACK #A3.
        ld      a, 7
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_length)
        ld      a, l
        call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
        jp      c, .upload_failed
        ld      a, h
        call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
        jp      c, .upload_failed
        call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
        jp      c, .upload_failed
        cp      NGS_BOOTSTRAP_LENGTH_ACK
        jp      nz, .upload_failed

        ; Phase 8: stream the custom firmware to RAM address $0000.
        ld      a, 8
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_source)
        ld      de, (neo_fw_length)
.firmware_stream_loop:
        ld      a, d
        or      e
        jr      z, .firmware_stream_done

        ; Refill the same 256-byte host buffer only after its current bytes
        ; have all been consumed. DE (firmware bytes left) survives the read.
        ld      bc, (neo_fw_buffer_remaining)
        ld      a, b
        or      c
        jr      nz, .firmware_buffer_ready
        push    de
        call    neo_fw_read_next_sector
        pop     de
        jp      c, .upload_failed
        ld      hl, 256
        ld      (neo_fw_buffer_remaining), hl
        ld      hl, #C000
.firmware_buffer_ready:
        ld      a, (hl)
        call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
        jp      c, .upload_failed
        inc     hl
        dec     de
        ld      (neo_fw_source), hl
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_buffer_remaining)
        dec     hl
        ld      (neo_fw_buffer_remaining), hl
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_source)
        jr      .firmware_stream_loop

.firmware_stream_done:
        ; Phase 9: new firmware has jumped to $0000 and sends ACK #A2.
        ld      a, 9
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
        jp      c, .upload_failed
        cp      NGS_FIRMWARE_READY_ACK
        jp      nz, .upload_failed

        ; Cross-check every GS data byte acknowledged by DBIT. The count
        ; includes #14's remaining three header bytes, bootstrap payload,
        ; #13's high address byte, firmware length (two bytes), and firmware.
        ; The first argument byte of #14 and #13 is preloaded before command
        ; dispatch and is therefore intentionally not counted.
        ld      a, 10
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
        ld      de, (neo_fw_length)
        add     hl, de
        ld      de, 6
        add     hl, de
        ld      de, (neo_fw_bytes_sent)
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      nz, .upload_failed

        xor     a
        ret

.upload_failed:
        scf
        ret



; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
; V2 staged upload. Package:
;   WORD stage0_length, stage0, "V2S1", WORD stage1_length, stage1, P124 payload
; The V1 upload path above remains unchanged and is selected when V2S1 is absent.
; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
upload_firmware_v2:
        ; Validate the V2 stage-0 first-sector layout.
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
        ld      a, h
        or      a
        jp      nz, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        ld      a, l
        or      a
        jp      z, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        cp      249
        jp      nc, upload_firmware_v2_failed

        ; Stage-0 source is the bytes immediately after the outer WORD.
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_package_source)
        inc     hl
        inc     hl
        ld      (neo_fw_bootstrap_source), hl

        ; Locate stage-1 length after the four-byte V2S1 marker.
        ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
        add     hl, de
        ld      de, 4
        add     hl, de
        ld      e, (hl)
        inc     hl
        ld      d, (hl)
        ld      (neo_fw_stage1_length), de

        ; Stage-1 receiver must fit in temporary WIN1 #4800..#4FFF.
        ld      a, d
        cp      8
        jr      c, .v2_stage1_high_ok
        jp      nz, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        ld      a, e
        or      a
        jp      nz, upload_firmware_v2_failed
.v2_stage1_high_ok:
        ld      a, d
        or      e
        jp      z, upload_firmware_v2_failed

        ; Stage-1 source starts immediately after its length word.
        inc     hl
        inc     hl
        ld      (neo_fw_stage1_source), hl
        ld      de, (neo_fw_stage1_length)
        add     hl, de
        ld      (neo_fw_source), hl       ; v2 payload follows stage-1 exactly

        ; The first sector has consumed 2 + stage0 + 4-byte marker + 2-byte
        ; stage1 length. The remaining bytes are streamed from this buffer.
        ld      hl, 248
        ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        ld      (neo_fw_buffer_remaining), hl

        ; Payload length = package length - (stage0 + stage1 + 8 framing bytes).
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_package_length)
        ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        ld      de, (neo_fw_stage1_length)
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        ld      de, 8
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        ld      (neo_fw_length), hl
        ld      a, h
        cp      #C1
        jp      nc, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        ld      a, h
        cp      #C0
        jr      c, .v2_payload_length_ok
        ld      a, l
        cp      #1B
        jp      nc, upload_firmware_v2_failed
.v2_payload_length_ok:
        ld      a, h
        or      l
        jp      z, upload_firmware_v2_failed

        xor     a
        ld      (neo_fw_bytes_sent), a
        ld      (neo_fw_bytes_sent+1), a
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        in      a, (GSSTAT)
        ld      (neo_fw_initial_status), a
        ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a

        ; Phase 11: cold restart and verify at least 70 physical RAM pages.
        ld      a, 11
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        ld      a, GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART
        call    gs_boot_send_command_long
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        ld      a, 12
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        call    gs_boot_probe_rom
        jr      nc, .v2_stock_rom_ready
        ld      a, 11
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        ld      a, GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART
        call    gs_boot_send_command_long
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        ld      a, 12
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        call    gs_boot_probe_rom
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed
.v2_stock_rom_ready:

        ; Install stage-0 into fixed WIN1 at $4000 using ROM command #14.
        ld      a, 13
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        ld      hl, 0
        ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        ld      (neo_fw_block_length), hl
        ld      a, l
        call    gs_boot_write_command_argument
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        ld      a, GS_CMD_RECEIVE_BLOCK
        call    gs_boot_send_command
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        ld      a, (neo_fw_block_length+1)
        call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        xor     a
        call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        ld      a, #40
        call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed

        ; Send the complete stage-0 image.
        ld      a, 14
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_bootstrap_source)
        ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
.v2_stage0_loop:
        ld      a, d
        or      e
        jr      z, .v2_stage0_done
        ld      a, (hl)
        call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        inc     hl
        dec     de
        jr      .v2_stage0_loop
.v2_stage0_done:

        ; Enter stage-0 at $4000 with ROM command #13.
        ld      a, 15
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        xor     a
        call    gs_boot_write_command_argument
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        ld      a, GS_CMD_JUMP_ADDRESS
        call    gs_boot_send_command
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        ld      a, #40
        call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
        jr      nc, .v2_stage0_jump_tx_done

        ; Stage-0 can consume #40, start immediately and publish #A1
        ; before the host observes the brief DBIT-clear interval. In that
        ; case DBIT is set by the incoming ACK, not by an unconsumed TX byte.
        in      a, (GSSTAT)
        ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
        bit     B_DBIT, a
        jp      z, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        cp      NGS_BOOTSTRAP_READY_ACK
        jp      nz, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        ; The #A1 response proves the pending #40 was consumed.
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_bytes_sent)
        inc     hl
        ld      (neo_fw_bytes_sent), hl
        xor     a
        ld      (neo_fw_tx_wait_state), a
        jr      .v2_stage0_ready

.v2_stage0_jump_tx_done:
        ; Stage-0 #A1, stage1 length, then stage-0's #A3 acceptance.
        ld      a, 16
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        ld      (neo_fw_last_rx_byte), a
        cp      NGS_BOOTSTRAP_READY_ACK
        jp      nz, upload_firmware_v2_failed

.v2_stage0_ready:
        ; #A1 has been consumed; report the stage-1 length handshake phase.
        ld      a, 16
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_stage1_length)
        ld      a, l
        call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        ld      a, h
        call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        cp      NGS_BOOTSTRAP_LENGTH_ACK
        jp      nz, upload_firmware_v2_failed

        ; Stream stage-1 bytes using the same 256-byte #C000 buffer.
        ld      a, 17
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_stage1_source)
        ld      (neo_fw_source), hl
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_stage1_length)
        ld      (neo_fw_stream_remaining), hl
        call    neo_fw_v2_stream_bytes
        jr      nc, .v2_stage1_stream_tx_done

        ; As with stage-0, stage-1 may publish #A4 immediately after its
        ; final byte is consumed. Recover that early reverse-direction ACK.
        in      a, (GSSTAT)
        ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
        bit     B_DBIT, a
        jp      z, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        cp      #A4
        jp      nz, upload_firmware_v2_failed

        ; #A4 proves the pending final stage-1 byte was consumed.
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_bytes_sent)
        inc     hl
        ld      (neo_fw_bytes_sent), hl
        xor     a
        ld      (neo_fw_tx_wait_state), a

        ; The stream helper advances its source counters only after a normal
        ; DBIT-clear observation. #A4 proves the final stage-1 byte was read,
        ; so account for that byte here before the payload stream starts.
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_source)
        inc     hl
        ld      (neo_fw_source), hl
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_buffer_remaining)
        dec     hl
        ld      (neo_fw_buffer_remaining), hl
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_stream_remaining)
        dec     hl
        ld      (neo_fw_stream_remaining), hl
        jr      .v2_stage1_ready

.v2_stage1_stream_tx_done:
        ; Stage-1 is now executing from WIN1/#4800 and signals #A4.
        ld      a, 18
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        ld      (neo_fw_last_rx_byte), a
        cp      #A4
        jp      nz, upload_firmware_v2_failed

.v2_stage1_ready:

        ; Send complete P124 payload length and require stage-1 #A3.
        ld      a, 19
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_length)
        ld      a, l
        call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        ld      a, h
        call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        cp      NGS_BOOTSTRAP_LENGTH_ACK
        jp      nz, upload_firmware_v2_failed

        ; Stream P124 header, descriptors and data unchanged.
        ld      a, 20
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_length)
        ld      (neo_fw_stream_remaining), hl
        call    neo_fw_v2_stream_bytes
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed

        ; Resident entry sends the established #A2 firmware-ready ACK.
        ld      a, 21
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
        jp      c, upload_firmware_v2_failed
        cp      NGS_FIRMWARE_READY_ACK
        jp      nz, upload_firmware_v2_failed

        ; Total DBIT-acknowledged bytes must equal the whole package length:
        ; #14 remainder 3 + stage0 + #13 high 1 + stage1 length 2 + stage1
        ; + payload length 2 + payload = stage0 + stage1 + payload + 8.
        ld      a, 22
        ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
        ld      de, (neo_fw_stage1_length)
        add     hl, de
        ld      de, (neo_fw_length)
        add     hl, de
        ld      de, 8
        add     hl, de
        ld      de, (neo_fw_bytes_sent)
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      nz, upload_firmware_v2_failed

        xor     a
        ret

upload_firmware_v2_failed:
        scf
        ret

; Stream neo_fw_stream_remaining bytes from neo_fw_source. The buffer pointer
; and remaining-byte count persist across the stage1 and payload calls.
neo_fw_v2_stream_bytes:
.v2_stream_loop:
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_stream_remaining)
        ld      a, h
        or      l
        jr      z, .v2_stream_done
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_buffer_remaining)
        ld      a, h
        or      l
        jr      nz, .v2_stream_buffer_ready
        call    neo_fw_read_next_sector
        ret     c
        ld      hl, 256
        ld      (neo_fw_buffer_remaining), hl
        ld      hl, #C000
        ld      (neo_fw_source), hl
.v2_stream_buffer_ready:
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_source)
        ld      a, (hl)
        call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
        ret     c
        inc     hl
        ld      (neo_fw_source), hl
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_buffer_remaining)
        dec     hl
        ld      (neo_fw_buffer_remaining), hl
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_stream_remaining)
        dec     hl
        ld      (neo_fw_stream_remaining), hl
        jr      .v2_stream_loop
.v2_stream_done:
        or      a
        ret


; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
; Standard GS105a ROM transport primitives.
; Commands use CBIT; data bytes use DBIT. All waits are bounded.
; -------------------------------------------------------------------------

; Probe stock ROM by reading the three-byte RAM size returned by command #20.
gs_boot_probe_rom:
        ld      a, GS_CMD_GET_TOTAL_RAM
        call    gs_boot_send_command_long
        ret     c
        call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
        ret     c
        ld      (neo_fw_rom_ram_low), a
        call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
        ret     c
        ld      (neo_fw_rom_ram_mid), a
        call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
        ret     c
        ld      (neo_fw_rom_ram_high), a
        ; #20 reports total RAM as a 24-bit byte count. The banked
        ; firmware layout reserves physical pages 68 and 69, while the x86
        ; backend owns pages 4..67. Therefore RAM must contain at least
        ; 70 unique 16-KiB pages: 70 * #4000 = #118000 bytes.
        ; 2-MiB boards report >= #200000 and pass; smaller boards are rejected.
        ld      a, (neo_fw_rom_ram_high)
        cp      #11
        jr      c, .rom_probe_too_small
        jr      nz, .rom_probe_ok
        ld      a, (neo_fw_rom_ram_mid)
        cp      #80
        jr      c, .rom_probe_too_small
.rom_probe_ok:
        or      a
        ret
.rom_probe_too_small:
        scf
        ret

; Long command wait for #F4 cold restart and ROM self-test.
gs_boot_send_command_long:
        ld      (neo_fw_command_byte), a
        ld      a, (neo_fw_command_byte)
        out     (GSCOM), a
        push    de
        ld      d, 16
.long_outer:
        ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
.long_inner:
        in      a, (GSSTAT)
        ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
        bit     B_CBIT, a
        jr      z, .long_done
        dec     bc
        ld      a, b
        or      c
        jr      nz, .long_inner
        dec     d
        jr      nz, .long_outer
        pop     de
        scf
        ret
.long_done:
        pop     de
        or      a
        ret

gs_boot_send_command:
        ld      (neo_fw_command_byte), a
        ld      a, (neo_fw_command_byte)
        out     (GSCOM), a
        ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
.command_wait:
        in      a, (GSSTAT)
        ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
        bit     B_CBIT, a
        jr      z, .command_done
        dec     bc
        ld      a, b
        or      c
        jr      nz, .command_wait
        scf
        ret
.command_done:
        or      a
        ret

; Preload the first argument byte of GS105a commands #13/#14. Those
; handlers read this byte before clearing CBIT; later arguments use DBIT.
gs_boot_write_command_argument:
        ld      (neo_fw_tx_byte), a
        ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
.argument_wait_free:
        in      a, (GSSTAT)
        ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
        bit     B_DBIT, a
        jr      z, .argument_slot_free
        dec     bc
        ld      a, b
        or      c
        jr      nz, .argument_wait_free
        scf
        ret
.argument_slot_free:
        ld      a, (neo_fw_tx_byte)
        out     (GSDAT), a
        or      a
        ret

gs_boot_send_data_byte:
        ld      (neo_fw_tx_byte), a
        ld      (neo_fw_last_tx_byte), a
        ld      a, 1
        ld      (neo_fw_tx_wait_state), a
        ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
.data_wait_free:
        in      a, (GSSTAT)
        ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
        bit     B_DBIT, a
        jr      z, .data_slot_free
        dec     bc
        ld      a, b
        or      c
        jr      nz, .data_wait_free
        scf
        ret
.data_slot_free:
        ld      a, (neo_fw_tx_byte)
        out     (GSDAT), a
        ld      a, 2
        ld      (neo_fw_tx_wait_state), a
        ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
.data_wait_consumed:
        in      a, (GSSTAT)
        ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
        bit     B_DBIT, a
        jr      z, .data_consumed
        dec     bc
        ld      a, b
        or      c
        jr      nz, .data_wait_consumed
        scf
        ret
.data_consumed:
        push    hl
        ld      hl, (neo_fw_bytes_sent)
        inc     hl
        ld      (neo_fw_bytes_sent), hl
        pop     hl
        xor     a
        ld      (neo_fw_tx_wait_state), a
        or      a
        ret

gs_boot_receive_data_byte:
        ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
.receive_wait:
        in      a, (GSSTAT)
        ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
        bit     B_DBIT, a
        jr      nz, .receive_ready
        dec     bc
        ld      a, b
        or      c
        jr      nz, .receive_wait
        scf
        ret
.receive_ready:
        in      a, (GSDAT)
        ld      (neo_fw_last_rx_byte), a
        ; DBIT is shared by both directions. After reading a NeoGS byte,
        ; let the sender observe the cleared slot before the host writes
        ; the next byte; otherwise a fast turnaround can hide the clear edge.
        push    af
        push    bc
        ld      bc, #0400
.receive_turnaround_delay:
        dec     bc
        ld      a, b
        or      c
        jr      nz, .receive_turnaround_delay
        pop     bc
        pop     af
        or      a
        ret

; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
; Project 124 private-protocol waits (custom firmware already running).
; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
neogs_wait_command_done::
        ld      bc, IPC_CMD_TIMEOUT
.cmd_wait:
        in      a, (GSSTAT)
        bit     B_CBIT, a
        jr      z, .cmd_done
        dec     bc
        ld      a, b
        or      c
        jr      nz, .cmd_wait
        scf
        ret
.cmd_done:
        or      a
        ret

neogs_wait_command_done_long::
        push    de
        ld      d, #10
.cmd_long_outer:
        ld      bc, IPC_CMD_TIMEOUT
.cmd_long_inner:
        in      a, (GSSTAT)
        bit     B_CBIT, a
        jr      z, .cmd_long_done
        dec     bc
        ld      a, b
        or      c
        jr      nz, .cmd_long_inner
        dec     d
        jr      nz, .cmd_long_outer
        pop     de
        scf
        ret
.cmd_long_done:
        pop     de
        or      a
        ret

neogs_wait_data_byte::
        ld      bc, IPC_BYTE_TIMEOUT
.data_wait:
        in      a, (GSSTAT)
        bit     B_DBIT, a
        jr      nz, .data_ready
        dec     bc
        ld      a, b
        or      c
        jr      nz, .data_wait
        scf
        ret
.data_ready:
        in      a, (GSDAT)
        or      a
        ret

; TR-DOS NeoGS package loader state and fixed 8-character filename.
neo_fw_filename:        db "neoprog "
neo_fw_load_buffer:     dw 0
neo_fw_load_remaining:  db 0
neo_fw_buffer_remaining: dw 0
neo_fw_load_track:      db 0
neo_fw_load_sector:     db 0
neo_fw_file_error:      db 0
neo_fw_file_length:     dw 0

; Stage 2 package parser and transfer state.
neo_fw_package_source:  dw 0
neo_fw_package_length:  dw 0
neo_fw_bootstrap_source: dw 0
neo_fw_bootstrap_length: dw 0
neo_fw_stage1_source:  dw 0
neo_fw_stage1_length:  dw 0
neo_fw_stream_remaining: dw 0
neo_fw_source:          dw 0
neo_fw_length:          dw 0
neo_fw_bootstrap_address: dw #4000
neo_fw_block_length:    dw 0
neo_fw_rom_ram_low:     db 0
neo_fw_rom_ram_mid:     db 0
neo_fw_rom_ram_high:    db 0
neo_fw_command_byte:    db 0
neo_fw_tx_byte:         db 0
neo_fw_last_tx_byte:    db 0
; 0=idle/acknowledged, 1=waiting for a free host data slot, 2=waiting for NeoGS ACK.
neo_fw_tx_wait_state:   db 0
neo_fw_last_rx_byte:    db 0
neo_fw_bytes_sent:      dw 0
neo_fw_initial_status:  db 0
neo_fw_last_status:     db 0
neo_fw_timeout_phase:   db 0


; =========================================================================
; FILE: zx_main/x86_loader.asm
; =========================================================================
; =========================================================================
; PROJECT 124 — TR-DOS task loader + NeoGS DMA / IPC
; Hardware protocol is based on NeoGS Programmer's Guide v0.1.A and
; ports.inc v0.3. The application-level command IDs/frame are Project 124.
;
; DMA mapping used here (proven working configuration):
;   NeoGS physical DMA destination = $00D000
;   NeoGS Z80 logical DMA frame address = $5000 (default WIN1 page #03)
;   two-byte length header at $5000; x86 execution begins at $5002.
;
; Host DMA writes to Spectrum address $0000-$3FFF. For DMA WRITE there is
; NO dummy write. The dummy access described by the user is only for DMA READ.
; =========================================================================

; neogs_io.inc is included once by neogs_driver.asm before this module.

DMA_HOST_WINDOW    EQU #0000
DMA_WINDOW_LIMIT   EQU #3000

EXECUTE_X86_FILE_STAGE::
        ld      (P124_X86_REQUEST_FILENAME), hl
        ld      (P124_X86_REQUEST_TYPE), a
        ld      (P124_X86_LOADER_SP_BACKUP), sp
        ld      sp, #BFFF              ; buffer starts at #C000; stack below it

        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_dbg_stack_isolated

        ; -------------------------------------------------------------
        ; Stage 5.0: deterministic diagnostic before accessing TR-DOS.
        ; B8 AA BB 40 90 F4 = MOV AX,BBAA / INC AX / NOP / HLT.
        ; Expected result: status=0, AX=BBAB.
        ; -------------------------------------------------------------
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_ipc_diag_start

        ; Exercise unknown-command release and explicit firmware reset before DMA.
        ld      a, #FE
        out     (GSCOM), a
        call    neogs_wait_command_done
        jp      c, .diag_protocol_error

        ld      a, CMD_RESET
        out     (GSCOM), a
        call    neogs_wait_command_done
        jp      c, .diag_protocol_error

        ld      hl, x86_diag_program
        ld      bc, x86_diag_program_end - x86_diag_program
        call    neogs_send_dma_block
        jp      c, .diag_dma_error

        call    neogs_run_and_get_result
        jp      c, .diag_protocol_error

        ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
        or      a
        jp      nz, .diag_execution_error
        ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
        ld      de, #BBAB
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      nz, .diag_wrong_result

        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_ipc_diag_pass
        ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13

        ; -------------------------------------------------------------
        ; Extended interpreter/transport self-test.
        ; Each case is sent through the real CBIT -> DMA -> DBIT path.
        ; Expected non-zero interpreter statuses are deliberate test results.
        ; -------------------------------------------------------------
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_ipc_edge_start

        ld      hl, x86_diag_inc_wrap
        ld      bc, x86_diag_inc_wrap_end - x86_diag_inc_wrap
        xor     a
        ld      de, #0000
        call    neogs_run_diag_case
        jp      c, .diag_extended_fail

        ld      hl, x86_diag_nop_hlt
        ld      bc, x86_diag_nop_hlt_end - x86_diag_nop_hlt
        xor     a
        ld      de, #0000
        call    neogs_run_diag_case
        jp      c, .diag_extended_fail

        ld      hl, x86_diag_unsupported
        ld      bc, x86_diag_unsupported_end - x86_diag_unsupported
        ld      a, 2
        ld      de, #00CC
        call    neogs_run_diag_case
        jp      c, .diag_extended_fail

        ld      hl, x86_diag_truncated_mov
        ld      bc, x86_diag_truncated_mov_end - x86_diag_truncated_mov
        ld      a, 4
        ld      de, #0000
        call    neogs_run_diag_case
        jp      c, .diag_extended_fail

        ld      hl, x86_diag_fallthrough
        ld      bc, x86_diag_fallthrough_end - x86_diag_fallthrough
        ld      a, 4
        ld      de, #0000
        call    neogs_run_diag_case
        jp      c, .diag_extended_fail

        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_ipc_edge_pass

        ; -------------------------------------------------------------
        ; NeoGS x86 memory test: 64 pages plus word/segment addressing cases.
        ; Destructive only to the reserved x86 memory region.
        ; -------------------------------------------------------------
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_memtest_start
        call    neogs_mem_test_and_get_result
        jp      c, .memtest_transport_fail
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_memtest_result
        ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
        ld      l, a
        ld      h, 0
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 32
        ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
        ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
        or      a
        jp      nz, .diag_memtest_fail
        ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
        ld      de, 1
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      nz, .diag_memtest_fail
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_memtest_pass

        ; BIOS foundation POST. Uses the same command/result channel; DMA is untouched.
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_bios_post_start
        call    neogs_bios_post_and_get_result
        jp      c, .bios_post_transport_fail
        ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
        or      a
        jp      nz, .bios_post_fail
        ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
        ld      de, #B105
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      nz, .bios_post_fail
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_bios_post_pass

        ; -------------------------------------------------------------
        ; Stage 2A: 8086 real-mode INT/IRET frame and stack test.
        ; This validates IVT lookup, FLAGS/CS/IP push order, IF/TF clearing,
        ; IRET restoration, and SP balance. Guest handler execution is Stage 2B.
        ; -------------------------------------------------------------
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_int_test_start
        call    neogs_int_test_and_get_result
        jp      c, .int_test_transport_fail
        ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
        or      a
        jp      nz, .int_test_fail
        ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
        ld      de, #1A01
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      nz, .int_test_fail
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_int_test_pass

        ; -------------------------------------------------------------
        ; Stage 2B: execute a guest INT 20h handler through CS:IP, then IRET.
        ; The guest handler changes AX and returns to the caller's HLT.
        ; -------------------------------------------------------------
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_int_guest_start
        call    neogs_int_guest_test_and_get_result
        jp      c, .int_guest_transport_fail
        ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
        or      a
        jp      nz, .int_guest_fail
        ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
        ld      de, #1A02
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      nz, .int_guest_fail
        ; Stage 2B reports each regression group separately. These messages are
        ; emitted only after the complete guest image and all final state
        ; assertions have passed.
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_int_guest_mov_flags_pass
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_int_guest_flow_pass
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_int_guest_stack_pass
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_int_guest_far_trace_pass
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_int_guest_alu_pass
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_int_guest_alu_memory_pass
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_int_guest_alu8_logic_pass
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_int_guest_alu8_memory_pass
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_int_guest_logic_memory_pass
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_int_guest_mov_memory_pass
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_int_guest_mov_address_pass
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_int_guest_io_pass
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_int_guest_strings_pass
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_int_guest_rep_strings_pass
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_int_guest_iret_pass

        ; -------------------------------------------------------------
        ; Stage 3A: guest INT 10h video BIOS services and live console stream.
        ; The NeoGS sends each teletype byte over DBIT while this host drains
        ; the stream to port #EF and the 42x12 6x8 debug window.
        ; -------------------------------------------------------------
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_bios_video_start
        call    neogs_bios_video_test_and_get_result
        jp      c, .bios_video_transport_fail
        ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
        or      a
        jp      nz, .bios_video_fail
        ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
        ld      de, #1A10
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      nz, .bios_video_fail
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_bios_video_pass

        ; -------------------------------------------------------------
        ; Stage 5A: search TR-DOS directory entry.
        ; Name #5CDD..#5CE4, type #5CE5, per the working project path.
        ; -------------------------------------------------------------
        ld      hl, (P124_X86_REQUEST_FILENAME)
        ld      de, #5CDD
        ld      bc, 8
        ldir
        ld      a, (P124_X86_REQUEST_TYPE)
        ld      (#5CE5), a
        ld      a, 9
        ld      (#5D06), a
        ld      c, 10
        call    #3D13
        ld      a, c
        cp      #FF
        jp      z, .file_not_found

        ; C=#0A returns only the catalogue entry number. Fetch the full
        ; 16-byte descriptor explicitly with C=#08 before using #5CE8..#5CEC.
        ld      a, c
        push    af
        xor     a
        ld      (#5D0F), a
        ld      (#5D10), a
        pop     af
        ld      c, 8
        call    #3D13
        ld      a, (#5D0F)
        ld      b, a
        ld      a, (#5D10)
        or      b
        jp      nz, .bad_file_descriptor

        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_file_ok

        ; -------------------------------------------------------------
        ; Stage 5B: validate descriptor BEFORE reading any sector.
        ; #5CE8 = byte length, #5CEA = sector count,
        ; #5CEB = first internal sector (0..15), #5CEC = track.
        ; The DMA frame window is $5000..$7FFF (12 KiB). Its first two
        ; bytes are the length header, so executable payload max is $2FFE.
        ; -------------------------------------------------------------
        ld      bc, (#5CE8)
        ld      a, b
        or      c
        jp      z, .empty_file_error
        ld      (P124_X86_FILE_LENGTH), bc

        ; Reject files larger than the executable 12 KB window minus header.
        ld      a, b
        cp      #2F
        jr      c, .file_length_ok
        jp      nz, .task_too_large
        ld      a, c
        cp      #FF
        jp      nc, .task_too_large
.file_length_ok:
        ; Sector count must equal ceil(file_length / 256).
        ld      hl, (P124_X86_FILE_LENGTH)
        ld      de, 255
        add     hl, de
        ld      e, h
        ld      a, (#5CEA)
        or      a
        jp      z, .empty_file_error
        cp      e
        jp      nz, .bad_file_descriptor
        ld      (P124_X86_READ_REMAINING), a

        ld      a, (#5CEB)
        cp      16
        jp      nc, .bad_file_descriptor
        ld      (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR), a
        ld      a, (#5CEC)
        ld      (P124_X86_READ_TRACK), a
        ld      hl, TRDOS_PANEL_BUFFER
        ld      (P124_X86_READ_BUFFER), hl

.read_sector_loop:
        ; Read exactly one sector per TR-DOS #05 call.
        ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_TRACK)
        ld      d, a
        ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR)
        ld      e, a
        ld      hl, (P124_X86_READ_BUFFER)
        ld      b, 1
        ld      c, 5
        xor     a
        ld      (#5D0F), a
        ld      (#5D10), a
        call    #3D13
        ; C=#05 stores its two-byte error result at #5D0F/#5D10.
        ld      a, (#5D0F)
        ld      b, a
        ld      a, (#5D10)
        or      b
        jp      nz, .sector_read_error

        ; Advance host buffer by 256 bytes.
        ld      hl, (P124_X86_READ_BUFFER)
        ld      de, 256
        add     hl, de
        ld      (P124_X86_READ_BUFFER), hl

        ; TR-DOS sectors are numbered 0..15 in this project convention.
        ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR)
        inc     a
        cp      16
        jr      c, .sector_same_track
        xor     a
        ld      (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR), a
        ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_TRACK)
        inc     a
        ld      (P124_X86_READ_TRACK), a
        jr      .sector_advanced

.sector_same_track:
        ld      (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR), a

.sector_advanced:
        ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_REMAINING)
        dec     a
        ld      (P124_X86_READ_REMAINING), a
        jp      nz, .read_sector_loop

        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_buf_loaded

        ; -------------------------------------------------------------
        ; Stage 5C: transfer actual task. The DMA frame contains a two-byte
        ; length prefix, therefore the total transfer must fit in $3000 bytes.
        ; -------------------------------------------------------------
        ld      bc, (P124_X86_FILE_LENGTH)
        ld      hl, TRDOS_PANEL_BUFFER
        call    neogs_send_dma_block
        jp      c, .task_dma_error
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_task_dma_ok

        ; -------------------------------------------------------------
        ; Stage 5D: execute and fetch framed result.
        ; -------------------------------------------------------------
        call    neogs_run_x86_real_and_get_result
        jp      c, .result_timeout

        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_x86_result_ready
        ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
        ld      l, a
        ld      h, 0
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 32
        ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13

        ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
        or      a
        jp      nz, .interpreter_error

        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_stack_guard_pass
        ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
        call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintHex16
        ld      a, 13
        call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar
        ld      a, 10
        call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar
        or      a
        jp      .loader_restore_stack

; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
; Error exits. All return through the same original-stack restoration.
; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
.diag_dma_error:
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_ipc_diag_dma_fail
        scf
        jp      .loader_restore_stack

.diag_protocol_error:
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_ipc_diag_frame_fail
        scf
        jp      .loader_restore_stack

.diag_execution_error:
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_ipc_diag_status_fail
        ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
        ld      l, a
        ld      h, 0
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_ipc_diag_opcode
        ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
        scf
        jp      .loader_restore_stack

.diag_extended_fail:
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_ipc_edge_fail
        scf
        jp      .loader_restore_stack

.bios_post_transport_fail:
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_bios_post_transport_fail
        scf
        jp      .loader_restore_stack

.bios_post_fail:
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_bios_post_fail
        ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
        ld      l, a
        ld      h, 0
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 32
        ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
        scf
        jp      .loader_restore_stack

.int_test_transport_fail:
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_int_test_transport_fail
        scf
        jp      .loader_restore_stack

.int_test_fail:
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_int_test_fail
        ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
        ld      l, a
        ld      h, 0
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 32
        ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
