        scf
        jp      .loader_restore_stack

.int_guest_transport_fail:
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_int_guest_transport_fail
        scf
        jp      .loader_restore_stack

.int_guest_fail:
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_int_guest_fail
        ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
        ld      l, a
        ld      h, 0
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 32
        ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
        scf
        jp      .loader_restore_stack

.bios_video_transport_fail:
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_bios_video_transport_fail
        scf
        jp      .loader_restore_stack

.bios_video_fail:
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_bios_video_fail
        ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
        ld      l, a
        ld      h, 0
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 32
        ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
        scf
        jp      .loader_restore_stack

.memtest_transport_fail:
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_memtest_transport_fail
        scf
        jp      .loader_restore_stack

.diag_memtest_fail:
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_memtest_fail
        ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
        ld      l, a
        ld      h, 0
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 32
        ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
        scf
        jp      .loader_restore_stack

.diag_wrong_result:
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_ipc_diag_wrong_ax
        ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
        scf
        jp      .loader_restore_stack

.file_not_found:
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_err_no_file
        scf
        jp      .loader_restore_stack

.empty_file_error:
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_err_empty_file
        scf
        jp      .loader_restore_stack

.bad_file_descriptor:
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_err_bad_descriptor
        scf
        jp      .loader_restore_stack

.sector_read_error:
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_err_sector_read
        ld      hl, (#5D0F)
        MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
        MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
        scf
        jp      .loader_restore_stack

.task_too_large:
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_err_task_too_large
        scf
        jp      .loader_restore_stack

.task_dma_error:
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_err_dma_transfer
        scf
        jp      .loader_restore_stack

.result_timeout:
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_err_result_timeout
        scf
        jp      .loader_restore_stack

.interpreter_error:
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_err_interpreter_status
        scf
        jp      .loader_restore_stack

.loader_restore_stack:
        ld      hl, (P124_X86_LOADER_SP_BACKUP)
        ld      sp, hl
        ret

; =========================================================================
; NeoGS transport helpers — exact CBIT/DBIT meanings from Guide v0.1.A
; =========================================================================

; IN: HL=source in host RAM, BC=payload byte count (1..#2FFE).
; OUT: Carry clear after NeoGS armed DMA, consumed the host end marker,
;      and disabled DMA. Carry set on timeout.
;
; Protocol:
;   host OUT GSCOM,CMD_LOAD_X86 -> CBIT=1
;   NeoGS configures DMA, clears CLRCBIT -> CBIT=0 (READY)
;   host LDIR to #0000..#3FFF (no dummy write)
;   host OUT GSCOM,#FF -> CBIT=1 (end marker)
;   NeoGS disables DMA, clears CLRCBIT -> CBIT=0 (DONE)
neogs_send_dma_block:
        ld      a, b
        or      c
        jp      z, .dma_bad_length
        ld      a, b
        cp      #40
        jr      c, .dma_length_ok
        jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
        ld      a, c
        or      a
        jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
.dma_length_ok:
        ld      (P124_DMA_SOURCE), hl
        ld      (P124_DMA_LENGTH), bc

        ; Validate payload + 2-byte length prefix <= $3000.
        push    bc
        pop     hl
        ld      de, 2
        add     hl, de
        ld      a, h
        cp      #30
        jr      c, .dma_total_length_ok
        jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
        ld      a, l
        or      a
        jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
.dma_total_length_ok:
        ld      a, CMD_LOAD_X86
        out     (GSCOM), a
        call    neogs_wait_command_done
        ret     c

        ; LDIR is used for BOTH pieces of the frame. This is intentional:
        ; the DMA engine observes host memory-write cycles. The first LDIR
        ; writes the two-byte header to host addresses #0000-#0001; the second
        ; writes the payload to #0002 onward. No direct LD (nn),A writes and
        ; no temporary stack are needed.
        di
        ld      hl, (P124_DMA_LENGTH)
        ld      (P124_DMA_HEADER), hl
        ld      hl, P124_DMA_HEADER
        ld      de, DMA_HOST_WINDOW
        ld      bc, 2
        ldir

        ld      hl, (P124_DMA_SOURCE)
        ld      de, DMA_HOST_WINDOW + 2
        ld      bc, (P124_DMA_LENGTH)
        ldir

        ; End marker is a command-byte write; it is NOT a DMA data byte.
        ld      a, #FF
        out     (GSCOM), a
        call    neogs_wait_command_done
        ret

.dma_bad_length:
        scf
        ret

; Send RUN command and fetch the four-byte application result frame:
; A5 signature, status, AX low, AX high.
neogs_run_and_get_result:
        ld      a, CMD_RUN_X86
        out     (GSCOM), a
        call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
        ret     c
        jp      neogs_get_result_frame

; Execute the actual TR-DOS task with the CS:IP real-mode core.
neogs_run_x86_real_and_get_result:
        ld      a, CMD_RUN_X86_REAL
        out     (GSCOM), a
        call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
        ret     c
        jp      neogs_get_result_frame

; Stage 3A guest BIOS video test. Its output is drained while the NeoGS
; command remains active; DBIT bytes are never confused with the result frame.
neogs_bios_video_test_and_get_result:
        ld      a, CMD_BIOS_VIDEO_TEST
        out     (GSCOM), a
        call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
        ret     c
        jp      neogs_get_result_frame

; Wait for the RUN command while servicing the bidirectional data channel.
; Stream protocol: nonzero=character, 00 00=clear, 00 01 row col=set cursor.
; Each character is sent to emulator printer port #EF and rendered on screen.
neogs_wait_run_done_with_console:
        push    de
        ld      d, #10
.run_outer:
        ld      hl, IPC_CMD_TIMEOUT
.run_inner:
        in      a, (GSSTAT)
        bit     B_DBIT, a
        jr      z, .no_stream_byte
        in      a, (GSDAT)
        or      a
        jr      z, .stream_control
        push    af
        ld      bc, #00EF
        out     (c), a
        pop     af
        call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar
        jr      .run_inner

.stream_control:
        call    neogs_wait_data_byte
        jr      c, .run_timeout
        or      a
        jr      z, .stream_clear
        cp      1
        jr      nz, .run_inner
        call    neogs_wait_data_byte
        jr      c, .run_timeout
        ld      (P124_BIOS_STREAM_ROW), a
        call    neogs_wait_data_byte
        jr      c, .run_timeout
        ld      c, a
        ld      a, (P124_BIOS_STREAM_ROW)
        call    BIOS_DebugConsole_SetCursor
        jr      .run_inner

.stream_clear:
        call    BIOS_DebugConsole_Clear
        jr      .run_inner

.no_stream_byte:
        bit     B_CBIT, a
        jr      z, .run_done
        dec     hl
        ld      a, h
        or      l
        jr      nz, .run_inner
        dec     d
        jr      nz, .run_outer
.run_timeout:
        pop     de
        scf
        ret
.run_done:
        pop     de
        or      a
        ret

; Run the BIOS low-memory POST and fetch the existing four-byte result frame.
neogs_bios_post_and_get_result:
        ld      a, CMD_BIOS_POST
        out     (GSCOM), a
        call    neogs_wait_command_done_long
        ret     c
        jp      neogs_get_result_frame

; Run the 8086 interrupt entry/return architectural self-test.
neogs_int_test_and_get_result:
        ld      a, CMD_INT_TEST
        out     (GSCOM), a
        call    neogs_wait_command_done_long
        ret     c
        jp      neogs_get_result_frame

; Run a real guest INT/IRET handler test and fetch the result frame.
neogs_int_guest_test_and_get_result:
        ld      a, CMD_INT_GUEST_TEST
        out     (GSCOM), a
        ; Drain NeoGS trace characters over DBIT while guest regression runs.
        ; The same host routine mirrors every byte to emulator port #EF.
        call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
        ret     c
        jp      neogs_get_result_frame

; Run the NeoGS physical-memory self-test and fetch its result frame.
neogs_mem_test_and_get_result:
        ld      a, CMD_MEM_TEST
        out     (GSCOM), a
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_memtest_command_sent
        call    neogs_wait_command_done_long
        jr      nc, .memtest_command_ack
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_memtest_command_timeout
        scf
        ret
.memtest_command_ack:
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_memtest_command_ack
        call    neogs_get_result_frame
        ret     nc
        MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_memtest_result_timeout
        scf
        ret

neogs_get_result_frame:
        ld      a, CMD_GET_STATUS
        out     (GSCOM), a

        call    neogs_wait_data_byte
        ret     c
        ld      (P124_X86_RESULT_MAGIC), a

        call    neogs_wait_data_byte
        ret     c
        ld      (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS), a

        call    neogs_wait_data_byte
        ret     c
        ld      e, a

        call    neogs_wait_data_byte
        ret     c
        ld      d, a
        ld      (P124_X86_RESULT_AX), de
        ld      (X86_RESULT_AX), de

        call    neogs_wait_command_done
        ret     c

        ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_MAGIC)
        cp      NGS_RESULT_SIGNATURE
        jr      z, .result_frame_ok
        scf
        ret
.result_frame_ok:
        or      a
        ret

; Diagnostic data: MOV AX,#BBAA / INC AX / NOP / HLT -> AX=#BBAB.
P124_msg_memtest_start: DEFB "[MEM] START: 64-page RAM map + word/segment tests.", 13, 0
P124_msg_memtest_command_sent: DEFB "[MEM] CMD_MEM_TEST sent; waiting for NeoGS completion...", 13, 0
P124_msg_memtest_command_ack: DEFB "[MEM] NeoGS command completed; requesting result frame.", 13, 0
P124_msg_memtest_command_timeout: DEFB "[MEM] STOP: command completion timeout (NeoGS did not release CBIT).", 13, 0
P124_msg_memtest_result_timeout: DEFB "[MEM] STOP: result-frame timeout after command completion.", 13, 0
P124_msg_memtest_result: DEFB "[MEM] RESULT status/AX = ", 0
P124_msg_memtest_transport_fail: DEFB "[MEM] FAIL: transport stopped before result frame.", 13, 0
P124_msg_memtest_pass:  DEFB "[MEM] PASS: 64 pages + word access + segment/offset wrap verified.", 13, 0
P124_msg_bios_post_start: DEFB "[BIOS] Stage 1: initialize IVT and BIOS Data Area...", 13, 0
P124_msg_bios_post_pass: DEFB "[BIOS] PASS: IVT/BDA initialized; 640 KiB and text profile verified.", 13, 0
P124_msg_bios_post_transport_fail: DEFB "[BIOS] FAIL: POST command/result timeout.", 13, 0
P124_msg_bios_post_fail: DEFB "[BIOS] FAIL: POST status/detail = ", 0
P124_msg_int_test_start: DEFB "[INT] Stage 2A: checking IVT lookup and 8086 interrupt stack frame...", 13, 0
P124_msg_int_test_pass: DEFB "[INT] PASS: FLAGS/CS/IP frame, IF/TF clear, IRET restore and SP balance.", 13, 0
P124_msg_int_test_transport_fail: DEFB "[INT] FAIL: interrupt test command/result timeout.", 13, 0
P124_msg_int_test_fail: DEFB "[INT] FAIL: status/detail = ", 0
P124_msg_int_guest_start: DEFB "[INT] Stage 2B: executing guest INT 20h handler and IRET...", 13, 0
P124_msg_int_guest_mov_flags_pass: DEFB "[CPU] PASS: MOV r16, INC/DEC and OF/SF/ZF/AF/PF; CF preservation verified.", 13, 0
P124_msg_int_guest_flow_pass: DEFB "[CPU] PASS: LOOP/JCXZ/JMP and taken/not-taken Jcc paths; FLAGS preserved.", 13, 0
P124_msg_int_guest_stack_pass: DEFB "[CPU] PASS: PUSH/POP, near CALL/RET, PUSH SP, POP SP and SS:FFFF wrap.", 13, 0
P124_msg_int_guest_far_trace_pass: DEFB "[CPU] TRACE PASS: CALL 0001:4FFE; RETF 0000:5008; JMP 0001:5000; SP=0100.", 13, 0
P124_msg_int_guest_alu_pass: DEFB "[CPU] PASS: 16-bit ADD/ADC/SUB/SBB/CMP register ModR/M; all six flags verified.", 13, 0
P124_msg_int_guest_alu_memory_pass: DEFB "[CPU] PASS: 16-bit arithmetic memory ModR/M; DS/SS and ES/CS/SS/DS overrides verified.", 13, 0
P124_msg_int_guest_alu8_logic_pass: DEFB "[CPU] PASS: 8-bit ADD/ADC/SUB/SBB/CMP; 8/16-bit AND/OR/XOR/TEST; flags verified.", 13, 0
P124_msg_int_guest_alu8_memory_pass: DEFB "[CPU] PASS: 8-bit ADD/ADC/SUB/SBB/CMP memory ModR/M; all EA forms, displacements and segment overrides verified.", 13, 0
P124_msg_int_guest_logic_memory_pass: DEFB "[CPU] PASS: 8/16-bit OR/AND/XOR/TEST memory ModR/M; segment overrides and TEST no-writeback verified.", 13, 0
P124_msg_int_guest_mov_memory_pass: DEFB "[CPU] PASS: MOV r/m8,r8 and r/m16,r16; DS/SS addressing verified.", 13, 0
P124_msg_int_guest_mov_address_pass: DEFB "[CPU] PASS: MOV all 8 EA forms, signed disp8/disp16, segment overrides and offset wrap.", 13, 0
P124_msg_int_guest_io_pass: DEFB "[CPU-I/O] PASS: IN/OUT E4-E7, EC-EF; imm8/DX; byte/word; full DX; FLAGS preserved.", 13, 0
P124_msg_int_guest_strings_pass: DEFB "[CPU-STR] PASS: MOVS/CMPS/STOS/LODS/SCAS byte/word; segment override; DF +/-; FLAGS.", 13, 0
P124_msg_int_guest_rep_strings_pass: DEFB "[CPU-STR-02] PASS: REP MOVSB; REPE CMPSB; REPNE SCASB; CX=0; REP + SS override.", 13, 0
P124_msg_int_guest_iret_pass: DEFB "[INT] PASS: guest INT 20h handler executed; IRET returned to caller.", 13, 0
P124_msg_int_guest_transport_fail: DEFB "[INT] FAIL: guest execution command/result timeout.", 13, 0
P124_msg_int_guest_fail: DEFB "[INT] FAIL: guest execution status/detail = ", 0
P124_msg_bios_video_start: DEFB "[BIOS] Stage 3A: guest INT 10h video services and 6x8 console...", 13, 0
P124_msg_bios_video_pass: DEFB "[BIOS] PASS: INT 10h mode/cursor/teletype; 42-column stream rendered.", 13, 0
P124_msg_bios_video_transport_fail: DEFB "[BIOS] FAIL: INT 10h video stream/result timeout.", 13, 0
P124_msg_bios_video_fail: DEFB "[BIOS] FAIL: INT 10h status/detail = ", 0
P124_msg_memtest_fail:  DEFB "[MEM] FAIL: status / detail = ", 0

x86_diag_program:
        defb    #B8, #AA, #BB, #40, #90, #F4
x86_diag_program_end:

; Additional interpreter edge cases, each transferred through actual DMA.
x86_diag_inc_wrap:
        defb    #B8, #FF, #FF, #40, #F4       ; AX wraps to 0000
x86_diag_inc_wrap_end:
x86_diag_nop_hlt:
        defb    #90, #F4                      ; NOP followed by HLT
x86_diag_nop_hlt_end:
x86_diag_unsupported:
        defb    #CC                            ; unsupported opcode -> status 2
x86_diag_unsupported_end:
x86_diag_truncated_mov:
        defb    #B8, #34                       ; MOV AX,imm16 missing high byte
x86_diag_truncated_mov_end:
x86_diag_fallthrough:
        defb    #90                            ; IP reaches exclusive end -> status 4
x86_diag_fallthrough_end:

; Run one diagnostic payload through DMA, execute it, and compare both
; interpreter status and AX. IN: HL=payload, BC=length, A=expected status,
; DE=expected AX. OUT: carry clear on exact match, set on any transport/result
; mismatch. This deliberately accepts the expected error statuses above.
neogs_run_diag_case:
        ld      (P124_DIAG_EXPECT_STATUS), a
        ld      (P124_DIAG_EXPECT_AX), de
        call    neogs_send_dma_block
        ret     c
        call    neogs_run_and_get_result
        ret     c
        ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
        ld      b, a
        ld      a, (P124_DIAG_EXPECT_STATUS)
        cp      b
        jr      nz, .diag_case_failed
        ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
        ld      de, (P124_DIAG_EXPECT_AX)
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jr      nz, .diag_case_failed
        or      a
        ret
.diag_case_failed:
        scf
        ret

; Data/state
P124_X86_LOADER_SP_BACKUP: dw 0
P124_DMA_SOURCE:           dw 0
P124_DMA_LENGTH:           dw 0
P124_DMA_HEADER:           dw 0
P124_X86_REQUEST_FILENAME: dw 0
P124_X86_REQUEST_TYPE:     db 0
P124_X86_FILE_LENGTH:      dw 0
P124_X86_READ_BUFFER:      dw 0
P124_X86_READ_REMAINING:   db 0
P124_X86_READ_TRACK:       db 0
P124_X86_READ_SECTOR:      db 0
P124_X86_RESULT_MAGIC:     db 0
P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS:    db 0
P124_X86_RESULT_AX:        dw 0
P124_DIAG_EXPECT_STATUS:    db 0
P124_DIAG_EXPECT_AX:        dw 0
P124_BIOS_STREAM_ROW:       db 0

; Strings
P124_msg_dbg_stack_isolated: defb "[DBG] LOADER STACK AT #BFFF; TR-DOS BUFFER AT #C000.", 13, 0
P124_msg_ipc_diag_start:     defb "[IPC TEST] Starting documented CBIT/DBIT + DMA diagnostic.", 13, 0
P124_msg_ipc_diag_pass:      defb "[IPC TEST] PASS: x86 HLT completed, AX=#", 0
P124_msg_ipc_diag_dma_fail:  defb "[IPC TEST] FAIL: DMA ready/end handshake timed out.", 13, 0
P124_msg_ipc_diag_frame_fail:defb "[IPC TEST] FAIL: command/data frame timeout or bad signature.", 13, 0
P124_msg_ipc_diag_status_fail:defb "[IPC TEST] FAIL: x86 interpreter status is not zero: #", 0
P124_msg_ipc_diag_opcode:     defb " opcode/AX=#", 0
P124_msg_ipc_diag_wrong_ax:  defb "[IPC TEST] FAIL: expected AX=#BBAB, got #", 0
P124_msg_ipc_edge_start:      defb "[IPC TEST] Running 5 interpreter edge cases through DMA.", 13, 0
P124_msg_ipc_edge_pass:       defb "[IPC TEST] PASS: 5 interpreter edge cases; DMA/IPC stable.", 13, 0
P124_msg_ipc_edge_fail:       defb "[IPC TEST] FAIL: interpreter edge-case or repeated DMA test.", 13, 0
P124_msg_file_ok:            defb "[STAGE 5] [TRDOS] Task file found.", 13, 0
P124_msg_buf_loaded:         defb "[STAGE 5] [BUFFER] File sectors loaded into host RAM #C000.", 13, 0
P124_msg_task_dma_ok:        defb "[STAGE 5] [DMA] NeoGS acknowledged DMA completion.", 13, 0
P124_msg_x86_result_ready:  defb "[STAGE 5] [RESULT] Status / AX = ", 0
P124_msg_stack_guard_pass: defb "[STACK] PASS: 128-byte WIN1 guard intact; stack stayed above #4880.", 13, 0
P124_msg_err_no_file:        defb "[STAGE 5] [ERROR] Task file not found.", 13, 0
P124_msg_err_empty_file:     defb "[STAGE 5] [ERROR] Task file is empty or has zero sectors.", 13, 0

P124_msg_err_sector_read:    defb "[STAGE 5] [ERROR] TR-DOS sector read failed; code=#", 0

; =========================================================================
; FILE: zx_main/debug_system.asm
; =========================================================================
; =========================================================================
; ПРОЕКТ 124 — СИСТЕМА ОТЛАДКИ ПОРТА #EF (ИЗ ПРОЕКТА 123)
; СИСТЕМНЫЙ ПУТЬ: zx_main/debug_system.asm (ТОЛЬКО ХАРДВЕРНЫЙ ПОРТ #EF)
; =========================================================================

; --- ГЛОБАЛЬНЫЙ МАКРОС ДЛЯ ВЫВОДА СИМВОЛА В ПОРТ #EF ---
MACRO_EF_CHAR   MACRO   char
                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
                    PUSH    AF
                    PUSH    BC
                    LD      A, char
                    LD      BC, #00EF
                    OUT     (C), A
                    POP     BC
                    POP     AF
                ENDIF
                ENDM

; --- ГЛОБАЛЬНЫЙ МАКРОС ДЛЯ ВЫВОДА СТРОКИ В ПОРТ #EF ---
MACRO_EF_STRING MACRO   str_addr
                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
                    PUSH    HL
                    LD      HL, str_addr
                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
                    POP     HL
                ENDIF
                ENDM

; --- ГЛОБАЛЬНЫЙ МАКРОС ДЛЯ ДАМПА 16-БИТНОГО РЕГИСТРА В ПОРТ #EF ---
MACRO_EF_HEX16  MACRO   reg_hl
                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
                    PUSH    HL
                    PUSH    reg_hl
                    POP     HL
                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintHex16
                    POP     HL
                ENDIF
                ENDM

; --- ФИЗИЧЕСКИЕ ПОДПРОГРАММЫ ОТЛАДКИ ПОРТА #EF ---
                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1

; ПОДПРОГРАММЫ ГЕНЕРАЦИИ ТАЙМСТАМПА В ПОРТ ОТЛАДКИ #EF
DBG_Port_PrintTimestamp::
        PUSH    AF
        PUSH    BC
        PUSH    HL
        
        CALL    READ_CURRENT_TIME       ; Считываем актуальное время из чипа К512ВИ1
        LD      BC, #00EF               ; Порт вывода отладки
        
        LD      A, "["
        OUT     (C), A
        LD      A, (txt_hour+0)
        OUT     (C), A
        LD      A, (txt_hour+1)
        OUT     (C), A
        LD      A, ":"
        OUT     (C), A
        LD      A, (txt_min+0)
        OUT     (C), A
        LD      A, (txt_min+1)
        OUT     (C), A
        LD      A, ":"
        OUT     (C), A
        LD      A, (txt_sec+0)
        OUT     (C), A
        LD      A, (txt_sec+1)
        OUT     (C), A
        LD      A, "]"
        OUT     (C), A
        LD      A, " "
        OUT     (C), A
        
        POP     HL
        POP     BC
        POP     AF
        RET

; Отправка ASCIIZ строки в порт #EF (с автоматическим таймстампом)
DBG_Port_PrintString::
        PUSH    AF
        PUSH    BC
        PUSH    HL
        
        CALL    DBG_Port_PrintTimestamp ; Принудительно выводим штамп времени

.loop_ef_s:
        LD      A, (HL)
        OR      A                       
        JR      Z, .done_ef_s
        LD      BC, #00EF
        OUT     (C), A
        INC     HL
        JR      .loop_ef_s
.done_ef_s:
        POP     HL
        POP     BC
        POP     AF
        RET

; Вывод 16-битного HEX-значения из HL в порт #EF
DBG_Port_PrintHex16::
        PUSH    AF
        PUSH    BC
        
        LD      A, H
        CALL    .local_hex8
        LD      A, L
        CALL    .local_hex8
        
        POP     BC
        POP     AF
        RET

.local_hex8:
        PUSH    AF
        RRA: RRA: RRA: RRA
        CALL    .local_nibble
        POP     AF
.local_nibble:
        AND     #0F
        CP      10
        JR      C, .is_dig
        ADD     A, 7
.is_dig:
        ADD     A, "0"
        LD      BC, #00EF
        OUT     (C), A
        RET

                ENDIF   ; Конец блока условной компиляции порта #EF


; =========================================================================


; =========================================================================
; FILE: zx_main/driver_cmos.asm
; =========================================================================
; =========================================================================
; PERFECT COMMANDER 123 — БЕЗОПАСНЫЙ ИСПРАВЛЕННЫЙ ДРАЙВЕР CMOS ЧАСОВ К512ВИ1
; СИСТЕМНЫЙ ПУТЬ: zx_main/driver_cmos.asm (ГЛОБАЛЬНЫЙ ЭКСПОРТ СИМВОЛОВ)
; =========================================================================

; Статические ASCII-буферы для хранения текстового представления времени
txt_hour::          DEFB "00"           
txt_min::           DEFB "00"           
txt_sec::           DEFB "00"

; =========================================================================
; ИНТЕЛЛЕКТУАЛЬНАЯ БЕЗОПАСНАЯ НАСТРОЙКА CMOS ЧАСОВ
; =========================================================================
CHECK_AND_INIT_CMOS::                   
        PUSH    AF: PUSH BC
        CALL    open_cmos
        
        ; Проверяем сигнатуру наличия часов в ячейке #11
        LD      A, #11
        CALL    read_byte_raw
        CP      #AA
        JR      Z, .already_active      
        
        ; Холодный старт: записываем обязательные системные константы
        LD      A, #11
        LD      L, #AA
        CALL    write_byte_raw         ; Маркер готовности системы
        
        LD      A, #0A
        LD      L, #20
        CALL    write_byte_raw         ; Регистр А: Включение кварца 32кГц
        
        LD      A, #0B
        LD      L, #02
        CALL    write_byte_raw         ; Регистр B: 24-часовой BCD формат

.already_active
        CALL    close_cmos
        POP     BC: POP     AF
        RET

; =========================================================================
; ПРОЦЕДУРА ЧТЕНИЯ ТЕКУЩЕГО ВРЕМЕНИ ИЗ ЧИПА RTC
; =========================================================================
READ_CURRENT_TIME::                     
        PUSH    AF: PUSH BC: PUSH HL
        DI
        CALL    open_cmos

.wait_rtc
        ; Контролируем флаг обновления UIP (битовая маска #80 в регистре #0A)
        LD      A, #0A
        CALL    read_byte_raw
        RLA                             
        JR      C, .wait_rtc            

        ; Вычитываем BCD данные с атомарным сохранением в текстовые буферы
        ; 1. Секунды (Ячейка #00)
        LD      A, #00
        CALL    read_byte_raw
        CALL    split_bcd
        LD      A, H: LD (txt_sec+0), A
        LD      A, L: LD (txt_sec+1), A

        ; 2. Минуты (Ячейка #02)
        LD      A, #02
        CALL    read_byte_raw
        CALL    split_bcd
        LD      A, H: LD (txt_min+0), A
        LD      A, L: LD (txt_min+1), A

        ; 3. Часы (Ячейка #04)
        LD      A, #04
        CALL    read_byte_raw
        CALL    split_bcd
        LD      A, H: LD (txt_hour+0), A
        LD      A, L: LD (txt_hour+1), A

        CALL    close_cmos
        EI
        POP     HL: POP BC: POP AF
        RET

; --- Атомарное низкоуровневое чтение байта из К512ВИ1 ---
read_byte_raw
        LD      BC, #DFF7
        OUT     (C), A                  
        LD      BC, #BFF7
        IN      A, (C)                  
        RET

; --- Атомарная низкоуровневая запись байта в К512ВИ1 ---
write_byte_raw
        LD      BC, #DFF7
        OUT     (C), A
        LD      BC, #BFF7
        OUT     (C), L
        RET

open_cmos
        LD      BC, #EFF7
        LD      A, #80
        OUT     (C), A
        RET

close_cmos
        LD      BC, #EFF7
        XOR     A
        OUT     (C), A
        RET

; --- Распаковка BCD полубайтов в ASCII символы ---
split_bcd
        PUSH    AF
        AND     %11110000               
        RRCA: RRCA: RRCA: RRCA
        ADD     A, 48
        LD      H, A
        POP     AF
        AND     %00001111               
        ADD     A, 48
        LD      L, A
        RET


; =========================================================================


; =========================================================================
; FILE: zx_main/log_debug.asm
; =========================================================================
; =========================================================================
; PERFECT COMMANDER 123 — СЕРВИСНЫЙ МОДУЛЬ МНОГОСТРОЧНОЙ ПЕЧАТИ И HEX-ЛОГОВ
; СИСТЕМНЫЙ ПУТЬ: zx_main/log_debug.asm (ПОЛНЫЙ КОД ЦЕЛИКОМ)
; =========================================================================

LOG_WriteMessageToBuffer::
        PUSH    AF: PUSH BC: PUSH DE: PUSH HL: PUSH IX
        PUSH    HL: POP IX                      

.loop_text:
        LD      A, (IX+0): OR A: JR Z, .msg_finished
        LD      B, A                    

        ; Отправляем символ напрямую в физический знакоместный рендерер экрана
        CALL    Direct_PrintChar           
        INC     IX: JR .loop_text

.msg_finished:
        ; Автоматически закрываем строку лога переводом каретки CR/LF
        CALL    LOG_CloseActiveLine

        POP     IX: POP HL: POP DE: POP BC: POP AF
        RET

LOG_CloseActiveLine::
        PUSH    AF: PUSH BC: PUSH DE: PUSH HL
        LD      A, #0D: CALL Direct_PrintChar
        LD      A, #0A: CALL Direct_PrintChar
        POP     HL: POP DE: POP BC: POP AF
        RET

; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
; ВЫВОД 16-БИТНОГО HEX-ЗНАЧЕНИЯ ИЗ HL НА ЭКРАН ХОСТА
; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
Util_PrintHex16Screen::
        PUSH    AF
        PUSH    BC
        LD      A, H
        CALL    Util_Internal_ScreenHex8   ; Печатаем старший байт (H)
        LD      A, L
        CALL    Util_Internal_ScreenHex8   ; Печатаем младший байт (L)
        POP     BC
        POP     AF
        RET

; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
; ВЫВОД 8-БИТНОГО HEX-ЗНАЧЕНИЯ ИЗ L НА ЭКРАН ХОСТА
; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
Util_PrintHex8Screen::
        PUSH    AF
        PUSH    BC
        LD      A, L
        CALL    Util_Internal_ScreenHex8   ; Печатаем байт напрямую
        POP     BC
        POP     AF
        RET

; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
; ГЛОБАЛЬНАЯ ПОДПРОГРАММА ПОБАЙТОВОГО РЕНДЕРА И ВЫВОДА В HEX НА ЭКРАН
; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
Util_Internal_ScreenHex8::
        PUSH    AF
        RRA: RRA: RRA: RRA                 ; Сдвигаем старший ниббл вниз
        CALL    .local_nibble
        POP     AF
.local_nibble:
        AND     #0F                        ; Изолируем 4 бита
        CP      10
        JR      C, .is_digit
        ADD     A, 7                       ; Коррекция для символов A..F
.is_digit:
        ADD     A, "0"                     ; Перевод в ASCII-код
        CALL    Direct_PrintChar           ; Печать символа в знакоместо экрана
        RET


; =========================================================================


; =========================================================================


; =========================================================================
; FILE: zx_main/display.asm
; =========================================================================
; =========================================================================
; PROJECT 124 — LOW LEVEL GRAPHIC CHR RENDERER (FROM PROJECT 123)
; СИСТЕМНЫЙ ПУТЬ: zx_main/display.asm (ПОЛНЫЙ КОД ЦЕЛИКОМ)
; =========================================================================

; =========================================================================
; [Дописать в самое начало файла zx_main/display.asm]
; =========================================================================
; Связываем имя шрифта из 123-го ядра с бинарным массивом ui_engine проекта 124
EXTERNAL_FONT_BIN EQU FONT_DATA_88


; --- ПРОЦЕДУРА ПРЯМОЙ ПЕЧАТИ НИЖНЕГО ОТЛАДЧИКА С АВТО-СКРОЛЛОМ ---
Direct_PrintChar::
        PUSH    BC
        PUSH    DE
        PUSH    HL
        LD      B, A                    

        ; Тотальный аппаратный перехват в физический порт отладки #EF
        IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
        PUSH    BC
        LD      BC, #00EF
        OUT     (C), A                  
        POP     BC
        ENDIF

        CP      #0D                     ; Проверяем возврат каретки (CR)
        JR      NZ, .check_lf
        XOR     A
        LD      (Console_X), A          
        JP      .done_print             

.check_lf:
        CP      #0A                     ; Проверяем перевод строки (LF)
        JR      NZ, .render_gate
        XOR     A
        LD      (Console_X), A          
        LD      A, (Console_Y)
        INC     A                       
        LD      (Console_Y), A
        CALL    EnsureConsoleSpace      
        JP      .done_print             

.render_gate:
        LD      A, (Console_Y)
        LD      D, A                    
        LD      A, (Console_X)
        LD      C, A                    
        
        LD      A, B                    
        CALL    Direct_PrintChar_Args   

        LD      A, (Console_X)
        INC     A
        LD      (Console_X), A          

        CP      32                      ; Автоперенос по правому краю
        JR      C, .done_print          
        
        XOR     A
        LD      (Console_X), A          
        LD      A, (Console_Y)
        INC     A                       
        LD      (Console_Y), A
        CALL    EnsureConsoleSpace      

.done_print:
        POP     HL
        POP     DE
        POP     BC
        RET

; --- ТОЧНЫЙ КAНОНИЧЕСКИЙ РЕНДЕРЕР ЗНAКОМЕСТА ИЗ ПРОЕКТА 123 ---
Direct_PrintChar_Args::
        PUSH    AF
        PUSH    BC
        PUSH    DE
        PUSH    HL

        ; 1. РАСЧЕТ ТОЧНОГО АДРЕСА АТРИБУТА ЦВЕТА (#5800)
        LD      A, D                    ; Console_Y (0..23)
        AND     #18                     
        RRCA: RRCA: RRCA
        ADD     A, #58                  
        LD      H, A
        
        LD      A, D
        AND     #07
        RLCA: RLCA: RLCA: RLCA: RLCA    
        LD      L, A
        
        LD      A, C                    ; Console_X (0..31)
        ADD     A, L
        LD      L, A                    

        LD      A, D                    
        CP      12                      
        JR      NC, .set_black_bg
        LD      A, %01001111            ; Верх (0..11): PAPER=1(Синий), INK=7(Белый), BRIGHT=1
        JR      .write_color
.set_black_bg:
        LD      A, %01000100            ; Низ (12..23): PAPER=0(Черный), INK=4(Зеленый), BRIGHT=1
.write_color:
        LD      (HL), A                 

        ; 2. КAНОНИЧЕСКИЙ РАСЧЕТ АДРЕСА ПИКСЕЛЕЙ В ЭКРАНЕ (#4000)
        LD      A, D                    
        AND     #18
        ADD     A, #40                  
        LD      H, A                    
        
        LD      A, D                    
        AND     #07
        RRCA: RRCA: RRCA
        LD      L, A
        
        LD      A, L
        AND     #E0
        ADD     A, C                    
        LD      E, A
        
        LD      A, L
        AND     #07
        OR      H
        LD      D, A                    ; DE = базовый адрес пикселей на экране

        ; 3. ПОСТРОЧНЫЙ РЕНДЕР ШРИФТА ИЗ ВЫБРАННОГО МАССИВА ОБОЛОЧКИ
        LD      A, B                    
        SUB     32
        LD      L, A
        LD      H, 0
        ADD     HL, HL
        ADD     HL, HL
        ADD     HL, HL                  ; HL = смещение буквы (ASCII * 8)
        
        PUSH    DE
        LD      A, (UI_Active_Font)
        AND     A
        JR      NZ, .low_font68
        LD      BC, FONT_DATA_88
        JR      .low_font_done
.low_font68:
        LD      BC, FONT_DATA_68
.low_font_done:
        ADD     HL, BC                  
        POP     DE

        ; Отрисовка 8 вертикальных линий
        LD      B, 8
.draw_font_loop:
        LD      A, (HL)
        LD      (DE), A
        INC     HL
        INC     D                       
        DJNZ    .draw_font_loop

        POP     HL
        POP     DE
        POP     BC
        POP     AF
        RET

; Отрисовка разделительной рамки панелей Commander
DrawCommanderPanels::
        PUSH    AF: PUSH BC: PUSH DE: PUSH HL
        LD      A, 11
        LD      (Console_Y), A
        XOR     A
        LD      (Console_X), A
        LD      B, 32
.loop_line:
        LD      A, #44                  ; Код горизонтальной линии "═"
        CALL    Direct_PrintChar_Args
        LD      A, (Console_X): INC A: LD (Console_X), A
        DJNZ    .loop_line
        POP     HL: POP DE: POP BC: POP AF
        RET


; =========================================================================


; =========================================================================
; FILE: zx_main/bios_console.asm
; =========================================================================
; =========================================================================
; PROJECT 124 — BIOS 6x8 debug console backend
; Lower Spectrum window: 42 columns x 12 rows, 6 pixels x 8 scanlines.
; Uses font68.bin (96 glyphs, 8 bytes per glyph). This renderer is separate
; from Direct_PrintChar so existing 32-column Commander panels are unchanged.
; =========================================================================

BIOS_DEBUG_COLUMNS EQU 42
BIOS_DEBUG_ROWS    EQU 12
BIOS_DEBUG_TOP     EQU 12

BIOS_DebugConsole_Init::
        ld      a, (Console_Y)
        sub     BIOS_DEBUG_TOP
        jr      nc, .row_ok
        xor     a
.row_ok:
        cp      BIOS_DEBUG_ROWS
        jr      c, .row_valid
        ld      a, BIOS_DEBUG_ROWS - 1
.row_valid:
        ld      (bios_dbg_row), a
        ld      a, (Console_X)
        cp      BIOS_DEBUG_COLUMNS
        jr      c, .col_valid
        xor     a
.col_valid:
        ld      (bios_dbg_col), a
        ret

; Clear only the lower 96-pixel-high window, not the Commander file panel.
BIOS_DebugConsole_Clear::
        push    af
        push    bc
        push    de
        push    hl
        ld      a, 96
        ld      (bios_dbg_clear_y), a
.clear_scanline:
        ld      a, (bios_dbg_clear_y)
        ld      c, 0
        call    bios_dbg_calc_addr
        push    hl
        pop     de
        inc     de
        ld      (hl), 0
        ld      bc, 31
        ldir
        ld      a, (bios_dbg_clear_y)
        inc     a
        ld      (bios_dbg_clear_y), a
        cp      192
        jr      c, .clear_scanline
        ld      hl, #5980
        ld      de, #5981
        ld      (hl), #44
        ld      bc, 383
        ldir
        xor     a
        ld      (bios_dbg_row), a
        ld      (bios_dbg_col), a
        ld      (Console_Y), a
        ld      a, BIOS_DEBUG_TOP
        ld      (Console_Y), a
        xor     a
        ld      (Console_X), a
        pop     hl
        pop     de
        pop     bc
        pop     af
        ret

; IN: A=row, C=column. Invalid coordinates are ignored.
BIOS_DebugConsole_SetCursor::
        cp      BIOS_DEBUG_ROWS
        ret     nc
        ld      (bios_dbg_row), a
        ld      a, c
        cp      BIOS_DEBUG_COLUMNS
        ret     nc
        ld      (bios_dbg_col), a
        ret

; IN: A=one output byte from the NeoGS stream.
; Printable bytes are drawn as packed 6-pixel glyphs; CR/LF/BS are handled.
BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar::
        push    af
        push    bc
        push    de
        push    hl
        ld      (bios_dbg_char), a

        cp      13
        jp      z, .carriage_return
        cp      10
        jp      z, .line_feed
        cp      8
        jp      z, .backspace
        cp      7
        jp      z, .done
        cp      32
        jp      c, .done
        cp      128
        jr      c, .glyph_range_ok
        ld      a, '?'
        ld      (bios_dbg_char), a
.glyph_range_ok:
        ld      a, (bios_dbg_char)
        sub     32
        ld      l, a
        ld      h, 0
        add     hl, hl
        add     hl, hl
        add     hl, hl
        ld      de, FONT_DATA_68
        add     hl, de
        ld      (bios_dbg_font_ptr), hl

        ; Eight scanlines share the same character-cell X origin.
        ; Reset the pixel coordinate for every glyph scanline.
        xor     a
        ld      (bios_dbg_scan), a

.glyph_scanline:
        ; Pixel X = column * 6. Recompute on EVERY scanline:
        ; glyph_pixel advances bios_dbg_xpixel by six pixels, so carrying
        ; that value into the next scanline causes diagonal glyph rendering.
        ld      a, (bios_dbg_col)
        add     a, a
        ld      c, a
        add     a, a
        add     a, c
        ld      (bios_dbg_xpixel), a

        ld      hl, (bios_dbg_font_ptr)
        ld      a, (hl)
        ld      (bios_dbg_bits), a
        inc     hl
        ld      (bios_dbg_font_ptr), hl

        ; Absolute Spectrum scanline = 96 + row*8 + scan.
        ld      a, (bios_dbg_row)
        add     a, a
        add     a, a
        add     a, a
        add     a, 96
        ld      b, a
        ld      a, (bios_dbg_scan)
        add     a, b
        ld      (bios_dbg_y), a

        ld      b, 6
.glyph_pixel:
        ld      a, (bios_dbg_xpixel)
        ld      c, a
        and     7
        ld      (bios_dbg_bitpos), a
        ld      a, c
        srl     a
        srl     a
        srl     a
        ld      c, a
        ld      a, (bios_dbg_y)
        call    bios_dbg_calc_addr

        ld      a, (bios_dbg_bitpos)
        ld      c, a
        ld      a, #80
.mask_loop:
        ld      d, a
        ld      a, c
        or      a
        ld      a, d
        jr      z, .mask_ready
        srl     a
        dec     c
        jr      .mask_loop
.mask_ready:
        ld      (bios_dbg_mask), a
        ld      a, (bios_dbg_bits)
        and     #20
        jr      z, .pixel_clear
        ld      a, (hl)
        ld      e, a
        ld      a, (bios_dbg_mask)
        or      e
        ld      (hl), a
        jr      .pixel_done
.pixel_clear:
        ld      a, (bios_dbg_mask)
        cpl
        ld      e, a
        ld      a, (hl)
        and     e
        ld      (hl), a
.pixel_done:
        ld      a, (bios_dbg_bits)
        add     a, a
        ld      (bios_dbg_bits), a
        ld      a, (bios_dbg_xpixel)
        inc     a
        ld      (bios_dbg_xpixel), a
        djnz    .glyph_pixel

        ld      a, (bios_dbg_scan)
        inc     a
        ld      (bios_dbg_scan), a
        cp      8
        jp      c, .glyph_scanline

        ld      a, (bios_dbg_col)
        inc     a
        ld      (bios_dbg_col), a
        cp      BIOS_DEBUG_COLUMNS
        jr      c, .done
        xor     a
        ld      (bios_dbg_col), a
        call    bios_dbg_newline
        jr      .done

.carriage_return:
        xor     a
        ld      (bios_dbg_col), a
        jr      .done
.line_feed:
        call    bios_dbg_newline
        jr      .done
.backspace:
        ld      a, (bios_dbg_col)
        or      a
        jr      z, .done
        dec     a
        ld      (bios_dbg_col), a
.done:
        pop     hl
        pop     de
        pop     bc
        pop     af
        ret

; IN: HL=16-bit value. Render four hexadecimal digits in the 6x8 console.
BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintHex16::
        push    af
        push    bc
        ld      a, h
        call    bios_dbg_print_hex8
        ld      a, l
        call    bios_dbg_print_hex8
        pop     bc
        pop     af
        ret

bios_dbg_print_hex8:
        push    af
        rra
        rra
        rra
        rra
        call    bios_dbg_print_nibble
        pop     af
bios_dbg_print_nibble:
        and     #0F
        cp      10
        jr      c, .hex_digit
        add     a, 7
.hex_digit:
        add     a, '0'
        jp      BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar

; Advance one 8-pixel character row and scroll the existing lower window.
bios_dbg_newline:
        ld      a, (bios_dbg_row)
        inc     a
        cp      BIOS_DEBUG_ROWS
        jr      c, .row_in_range
        call    UI_ScrollConsoleUp
        ld      a, BIOS_DEBUG_ROWS - 1
.row_in_range:
        ld      (bios_dbg_row), a
        ret

; IN: A=absolute Y (0..191), C=screen byte X (0..31).
; OUT: HL=Spectrum bitmap address for that scanline and byte.
bios_dbg_calc_addr:
        ld      d, a
        and     #C0
        rrca
        rrca
        rrca
        ld      h, a
        ld      a, d
        and     #07
        or      h
        or      #40
        ld      h, a
        ld      a, d
        and     #38
        rlca
        rlca
        add     a, c
        ld      l, a
        ; Start each pixel with a fresh mask = 80h >> (X & 7).
        ld      a, #80
        ld      (bios_dbg_mask), a
        ret

bios_dbg_row:       db 0
bios_dbg_col:       db 0
bios_dbg_char:      db 0
bios_dbg_scan:      db 0
bios_dbg_y:         db 0
bios_dbg_xpixel:    db 0
bios_dbg_bitpos:    db 0
bios_dbg_bits:      db 0
bios_dbg_mask:      db 0
bios_dbg_font_ptr:  dw 0
bios_dbg_clear_y:   db 0


; =========================================================================
; FILE: zx_main/ui_engine.asm
; =========================================================================
; =========================================================================
; PROJECT 124 — USER INTERFACE & GRAPHIC TEXT ENGINE (FROM PROJECT 123)
; СИСТЕМНЫЙ ПУТЬ: zx_main/ui_engine.asm (ПОЛНЫЙ КОД ЦЕЛИКОМ)
; =========================================================================

UI_Active_Font:: DB 0                    ; 0 = font88, 1 = font68

; --- ГЛОБАЛЬНЫЕ ПЕРЕМЕННЫЕ КУРСОРНОЙ НАВИГАЦИИ (ИЗ ПРОЕКТА 123) ---
CursorY::        DEFB 2                  ; Текущая строка курсора панели (2..10)

; --- ТAБЛИЧНЫЙ СKРOЛЛИНГ НИЖНЕГO OKНA ЛOГOВ ИЗ ПРOЕKТA 123 ---
UI_ScrollConsoleUp::
        PUSH    IX: PUSH AF: PUSH BC: PUSH DE: PUSH HL
        XOR     A                       
        LD      (.local_micro_ctr), A
.global_loop:
        LD      IX, .Line_Addr_Table
        LD      A, 11                   
        LD      (.local_row_ctr), A       
.rows_loop:
        LD      E, (IX+0): LD D, (IX+1)               
        LD      L, (IX+2): LD H, (IX+3)               
        PUSH    IX: PUSH BC                      
        LD      A, (.local_micro_ctr): ADD A, D: LD D, A                    
        LD      A, (.local_micro_ctr): ADD A, H: LD H, A                    
        LD      BC, 32: LDIR                            
        POP     BC: POP IX                      
        LD      DE, 4: ADD IX, DE                  
        LD      A, (.local_row_ctr): DEC A: LD (.local_row_ctr), A
        JR      NZ, .rows_loop   

        LD      A, (.local_micro_ctr): INC A: LD (.local_micro_ctr), A
        CP      8                        
        JR      C, .global_loop   

        ; Сдвиг цветовых атрибутов лога
        LD      DE, #5980: LD HL, #59A0: LD BC, 352: LDIR                            
        ; Очистка последней строки атрибутов
        LD      HL, #5AE0: LD DE, #5AE1: LD (HL), %01000100: LD BC, 31: LDIR                            
        ; Очистка пикселей 23-й строки
        XOR     A: LD H, #50                  
.clear_pixels:
        PUSH    AF: PUSH HL                      
        LD      L, #E0: LD C, 32: XOR A
.clear_bytes:
        LD      (HL), A: INC HL: DEC C: JR NZ, .clear_bytes  
        POP     HL: INC H: POP AF: INC A: CP 8: JR C, .clear_pixels

        POP     HL: POP DE: POP BC: POP AF: POP IX
        LD      A, 23: LD (Console_Y), A
        XOR     A: LD (Console_X), A
        RET

.Line_Addr_Table:
        DW #4880, #48A0, #48A0, #48C0, #48C0, #48E0, #48E0, #5000                 
        DW #5000, #5020, #5020, #5040, #5040, #5060, #5060, #5080                 
        DW #5080, #50A0, #50A0, #50C0, #50C0, #50E0                 

.local_micro_ctr: DB 0
.local_row_ctr:   DB 0

ClearScreenROM::
        PUSH    BC: PUSH DE: PUSH HL
        LD      HL, #4000: LD DE, #4001: LD (HL), 0: LD BC, 6143: LDIR                            
        LD      HL, #5800: LD DE, #5801: LD (HL), %01000100: LD BC, 767: LDIR
        POP     HL: POP DE: POP BC
        RET

ForceInterfaceColors::
        PUSH    AF: PUSH BC: PUSH DE: PUSH HL
        LD      HL, #5800
        LD      DE, #5801
        LD      (HL), %01001111         ; Верх: Синий
        LD      BC, 383                 
        LDIR
        
        LD      HL, #5980
        LD      DE, #5981
        LD      (HL), %01000100         ; Низ: Черный
        LD      BC, 383                 
        LDIR
        POP     HL: POP DE: POP BC: POP AF
        RET      

; --- АППАРАТНЫЙ ОПРОС КЛАВИШ НАВИГАЦИИ (ИЗ ПРОЕКТА 123) ---
GetHardwareKey::                        
        LD      BC, #DFFE: IN A, (C): BIT 1, A: JR Z, .key_o_pressed        
        LD      BC, #BFFE: IN A, (C): BIT 1, A: JR Z, .key_l_pressed        
        XOR     A: RET
.key_o_pressed: LD A, 1: RET
.key_l_pressed: LD A, 2: RET

; =========================================================================
; ИМПОРТ БИНАРНЫХ ФАЙЛОВ ШРИФТОВ ИЗ ВАШЕГО КАТАЛОГА
; =========================================================================
        ALIGN 256
FONT_DATA_88:
        INCBIN "font88.bin"             ; Крупный шрифт 8x8

        ALIGN 256
FONT_DATA_68:
        INCBIN "font68.bin"             ; Компактный шрифт 6x8


; =========================================================================


; =========================================================================
; FILE: zx_main/ui_panels.asm
; =========================================================================
; =========================================================================
; PROJECT 124 — FILE PANELS & HIGH LEVEL GRAPHICS (FROM PROJECT 123)
; СИСТЕМНЫЙ ПУТЬ: zx_main/ui_panels.asm (ПОЛНЫЙ КОД ЦЕЛИКОМ)
; =========================================================================

; --- ТАБЛИЦА АДРЕСОВ АТРИБУТОВ СТРОК КУРСOРА ПАНЕЛИ (Y = 2..10) ---
Cursor_Row_Table:
        DW #5841 , #5861 , #5881 , #58A1 , #58C1 , #58E1 , #5901 , #5921 , #5941

; --- ВЫЧИСЛЕНИЕ АДРЕСА СТРОКИ АТРИБУТОВ ДЛЯ КУРСОРНОЙ НАВИГАЦИИ ---
Get_Cursor_RAM_Address:
        LD      A, (CursorY)            
        SUB     2                       
        ADD     A, A                    
        LD      E, A 
        LD      D, 0 
        LD      HL, Cursor_Row_Table    
        ADD     HL, DE                  
        LD      A, (HL) 
        LD      E, A 
        INC     HL 
        LD      A, (HL) 
        LD      D, A 
        EX      DE, HL                  
        RET 

; --- ОТРЕСОВКА ИНВЕРСИОННОЙ ПОЛОСЫ ВЫДЕЛЕНИЯ ФАЙЛА ---
DrawActiveCursor::
        PUSH    BC                      
        PUSH    HL                      
        CALL    Get_Cursor_RAM_Address  
        LD      B, 30                   
.loop:
        LD      (HL), %00111000         ; Инверсия: Белый фон, черный text
        INC     HL                     
        DJNZ    .loop                  
        POP     HL                     
        POP     BC                      
        RET                             

; --- СТИРАНИЕ ИНВЕРСИОННОЙ ПОЛОСЫ С ВОЗВРАТОМ СИНЕГО ЦВЕТА ---
EraseActiveCursor::
        PUSH    BC                      
        PUSH    HL                      
        CALL    Get_Cursor_RAM_Address  
        LD      B, 30                   
.loop:
        LD      (HL), %01001111         ; Синий фон %4F
        INC     HL                     
        DJNZ    .loop                  
        POP     HL                     
        POP     BC                      
        RET                             

; --- ГЛОБАЛЬНАЯ ТОЧКА ИНИЦИАЛИЗАЦИИ ИНТЕРФЕЙСА ОКОН PERFECT COMMANDER ---
INIT_PROGRAM_INTERFACE::
        PUSH    AF: PUSH BC: PUSH DE: PUSH HL

        CALL    ClearScreenROM          ; Полная очистка экрана и атрибутов
        
        LD      A, 12                   
        LD      (#5C6A), A              ; DF_SZ = 12 строк для панели файлов
        LD      A, %00111000            
        LD      (#5C8D), A             
        LD      A, %01000100            
        LD      (#5C8C), A             

        XOR     A                       
        OUT     (254), A                ; Черный бордюр

        LD      A, %00111000            
        LD      (#5C48), A             
        LD      (#5C8F), A             
        LD      (#5C49), A             

        CALL    ForceInterfaceColors    ; Заливка окон цветом
        CALL    DrawCommanderPanels     ; Отрисовка разделительной линии панелей

        ; Начальные координаты для вывода логов в нижнем черном окне
        LD      A, 12                   
        LD      (Console_Y), A
        XOR     A
        LD      (Console_X), A         

        ; ОТРЕНДЕРИТЬ ДИНАМИЧЕСКИЙ КАТАЛОГ ДИСКА А
        CALL    Refresh_Panel_Directory 

        ; Фиксируем полосу курсора на первую строку файлов (строка 2)
        LD      A, 2                  
        LD      (CursorY), A          
        CALL    DrawActiveCursor       

        POP     HL: POP DE: POP BC: POP AF
        RET

; --- АППАРАТНЫЙ КОНТРОЛЬ СВОБОДНОГО МЕСТА ДЛЯ ВЕРТИКАЛЬНОГО СКРОЛЛИНГА ---
EnsureConsoleSpace::
        PUSH    AF
        PUSH    BC
        PUSH    DE
        PUSH    HL

        LD      A, (Console_Y)
        CP      24                      ; Нижний лог занимает строки 12..23
        JR      C, .space_ok            

        CALL    UI_ScrollConsoleUp      ; Вызываем табличный аппаратный сдвиг экрана
        
        LD      A, 23                   
        LD      (Console_Y), A
        XOR     A
        LD      (Console_X), A          

.space_ok:
        POP     HL
        POP     DE
        POP     BC
        POP     AF
        RET


; =========================================================================


; =========================================================================
; FILE: zx_main/file_utils.asm
; =========================================================================
; =========================================================================
; FILE: zx_main/file_utils.asm
; PROJECT 124 — TR-DOS directory utilities
; Sector API verified for TR-DOS 5.04T: C=#05, D=track, E=internal sector
; 0..15, B=count, HL=destination; error status at #5D0F.
; =========================================================================

; IN: CursorY = visible row (2..10)
; OUT: HL = 16-byte descriptor in TRDOS_PANEL_BUFFER, carry clear.
;      HL=0 and carry set for an invalid/empty row.
GetSelectedFileDescriptor::
        ld      a, (CursorY)
        cp      2
        jr      c, .bad_row
        cp      11
        jr      nc, .bad_row
        sub     2
        ld      l, a
        ld      h, 0
        add     hl, hl
        add     hl, hl
        add     hl, hl
        add     hl, hl
        ld      de, TRDOS_PANEL_BUFFER
        add     hl, de

        ; Reject an empty/deleted/invalid catalogue slot.
        ld      a, (hl)
        cp      32
        jr      c, .bad_row
        cp      127
        jr      nc, .bad_row
        or      a
        ret
.bad_row:
        ld      hl, 0
        scf
        ret

LogSelectedFile::
        push    hl
        ld      hl, .str_exec_prefix
        call    LOG_WriteMessageToBuffer
        pop     hl
        ret
.str_exec_prefix:
        defb "[LAUNCH] Invoking system CODE loader for selected workspace node...", #0D, #0A, 0

; Read and render the complete 128-entry TR-DOS catalogue (8 * 256 bytes).
PrintDiskDirectory::
        push    af
        push    bc
        push    de
        push    hl

        ; Select drive A and clear the two-byte TR-DOS error result.
        xor     a
        ld      (#5D19), a
        ld      (#5D0F), a
        ld      (#5D10), a

        ; C=#05 raw resident convention: D=track 0, E=sector 0..15.
        ld      de, #0000
        ld      b, 8
        ld      c, 5
        ld      hl, TRDOS_PANEL_BUFFER
        call    #3D13

        ; C=#05 reports read errors in the TR-DOS error byte #5D0F.
        ld      a, (#5D0F)
        ld      b, a
        ld      a, (#5D10)
        or      b
        jp      nz, .directory_read_error

        ld      b, 128
        ld      hl, TRDOS_PANEL_BUFFER
        ld      a, 2
        ld      (File_Y), a

.loop_slots:
        push    bc
        push    hl

        ld      a, (hl)
        or      a
        jp      z, .empty_slot
        cp      1
        jp      z, .invalid_slot
        cp      32
        jp      c, .invalid_slot
        cp      127
        jp      nc, .invalid_slot

        ld      a, 2
        ld      (File_X), a
        ld      c, 25
.clear_pixels_loop:
        push    bc
        push    hl
        ld      a, (File_X)
        ld      c, a
        ld      a, (File_Y)
        ld      d, a
        ld      b, " "
        call    Direct_PrintChar_Args
        pop     hl
        pop     bc
        ld      a, (File_X)
        inc     a
        ld      (File_X), a
        dec     c
        jr      nz, .clear_pixels_loop

        ld      a, 2
        ld      (File_X), a
        ld      c, 8
.print_name:
        ld      a, (hl)
        push    bc
        push    hl
        ld      b, a
        ld      a, (File_X)
        ld      c, a
        ld      a, (File_Y)
        ld      d, a
        call    Direct_PrintChar_Args
        ld      a, (File_X)
        inc     a
        ld      (File_X), a
        pop     hl
        pop     bc
        inc     hl
        dec     c
        jr      nz, .print_name

        ; Extension separator at X=10.
        ld      a, 10
        ld      (File_X), a
        push    hl
        ld      c, 10
        ld      a, (File_Y)
        ld      d, a
        ld      b, "."
        call    Direct_PrintChar_Args
        pop     hl

        ; File type at X=11.
        ld      a, 11
        ld      (File_X), a
        ld      a, (hl)
        push    hl
        ld      b, a
        ld      a, 11
        ld      c, a
        ld      a, (File_Y)
        ld      d, a
        call    Direct_PrintChar_Args
        pop     hl

        ; Descriptor byte 13 = allocated sector count. Display ceil(sectors/4)
        ; without 8-bit overflow: (sectors + 3) is calculated in 16 bits.
        push    hl
        ld      de, 13
        add     hl, de
        ld      a, (hl)
        ld      e, a
        ld      d, 0
        ld      hl, 3
        add     hl, de
        srl     h
        rr      l
        srl     h
        rr      l
        ld      a, l
        pop     hl

        ld      c, 16
        call    Util_PrintDecimalByte

        ld      a, 20
        ld      (File_X), a
        ld      a, (File_Y)
        ld      d, a
        ld      c, 20
        ld      b, "K"
        call    Direct_PrintChar_Args

        ld      a, 21
        ld      (File_X), a
        ld      a, (File_Y)
        ld      d, a
        ld      c, 21
        ld      b, "b"
        call    Direct_PrintChar_Args

        ld      a, (File_Y)
        inc     a
        ld      (File_Y), a
        cp      11
        jp      nc, .directory_done

        ; Advance to next 16-byte descriptor. Restore saved slot counter.
        pop     hl
        ld      de, 16
        add     hl, de
        pop     bc
        dec     b
        jp      nz, .loop_slots
        jp      .directory_success

.invalid_slot:
        ; Drop the per-slot saves, advance HL, and decrement the ORIGINAL
        ; 128-entry counter. Do not load 16 into BC (that destroys B), and
        ; do not push the per-slot saves again before re-entering the loop.
        pop     hl
        pop     bc
        ld      de, 16
        add     hl, de
        dec     b
        jp      nz, .loop_slots
        jp      .directory_success

.empty_slot:
        ; Per-slot BC/HL saves are still present.
        pop     hl
        pop     bc
        jp      .directory_success

.directory_done:
        ; Per-slot BC/HL saves are still present.
        pop     hl
        pop     bc
.directory_success:
        pop     hl
        pop     de
        pop     bc
        pop     af
        or      a
        ret

.directory_read_error:
        pop     hl
        pop     de
        pop     bc
        pop     af
        scf
        ret

; IN: A=value 0..255, C=screen X. Prints exactly three decimal digits.
Util_PrintDecimalByte:
        push    af
        push    bc
        push    de
        push    hl
        ld      e, a
        ld      a, c
        ld      (File_X), a
        ld      a, e
        ld      b, 0
.hundreds:
        cp      100
        jr      c, .p_hundreds
        sub     100
        inc     b
        jr      .hundreds
.p_hundreds:
        ld      e, a
        ld      a, b
        add     a, #30
        push    de
        ld      b, a
        ld      a, (File_X)
        ld      c, a
        ld      a, (File_Y)
        ld      d, a
        call    Direct_PrintChar_Args
        ld      a, (File_X)
        inc     a
        ld      (File_X), a
        pop     de

        ld      a, e
        ld      b, 0
.tens:
        cp      10
        jr      c, .p_tens
        sub     10
        inc     b
        jr      .tens
.p_tens:
        ld      e, a
        ld      a, b
        add     a, #30
        push    de
        ld      b, a
        ld      a, (File_X)
        ld      c, a
        ld      a, (File_Y)
        ld      d, a
        call    Direct_PrintChar_Args
        ld      a, (File_X)
        inc     a
        ld      (File_X), a
        pop     de

        ld      a, e
        add     a, #30
        ld      b, a
        ld      a, (File_X)
        ld      c, a
        ld      a, (File_Y)
        ld      d, a
        call    Direct_PrintChar_Args
        pop     hl
        pop     de
        pop     bc
        pop     af
        ret

Refresh_Panel_Directory::
        push    af
        push    bc
        push    de
        push    hl

        ; Fill 320 attribute bytes with #4F. The original byte sequence was
        ; LD HL,#5841, leaving DE undefined. This is the correct fill idiom.
        ld      hl, #5840
        ld      (hl), %01001111
        ld      de, #5841
        ld      bc, 319
        ldir

        call    PrintDiskDirectory
        jr      c, .refresh_failed
        call    DrawActiveCursor
        pop     hl
        pop     de
        pop     bc
        pop     af
        or      a
        ret
.refresh_failed:
        pop     hl
        pop     de
        pop     bc
        pop     af
        scf
        ret

File_X: DB 0
File_Y: DB 0


; =========================================================================
; FILE: neogs_sub/main.asm
; =========================================================================
; =========================================================================
; PROJECT 124 — NeoGS Z80 firmware / command dispatcher
; Based on NeoGS Programmer's Guide v0.1.A and ports.inc v0.3.
;
; MEMORY MAP / BOOT:
; WIN0 ($0000-$3FFF), PG0/page #00: fixed resident Z80 firmware.
; WIN1 ($4000-$7FFF), PG1/page #03: ROM-return trampoline at $4000,
;   Z80 stack at $4FFE with guard $4800-$487F, DMA frame at $5000-$7FFF.
; WIN2 ($8000-$BFFF), PG2 via MPAG: reserved for banked firmware overlays.
; WIN3 ($C000-$FFFF), PG3 via MPAGEX: x86 physical-memory window (pages 4..67).
; The resident stack is deliberately outside WIN2 and WIN3 so both windows
; can be bank-switched independently without moving the active Z80 stack.
; =========================================================================

        INCLUDE "../shared/neogs_io.inc"

        ORG     $0000

ROM_RETURN_TRAMPOLINE_ADDR EQU $4000
X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR        EQU $5000
X86_RAM_RUN_ADDR           EQU $5002
Z80_STACK_TOP              EQU $4FFE
Z80_STACK_GUARD_BASE       EQU $4800
Z80_STACK_GUARD_SIZE       EQU $0080
Z80_STACK_FILL_LENGTH      EQU Z80_STACK_TOP - Z80_STACK_GUARD_BASE - 1
DMA_WAIT_LIMIT   EQU #FFFF
DMA_WAIT_OUTER   EQU 8
IPC_WAIT_LIMIT   EQU #FFFF

; Firmware entry vector
        jp      start_neoprogram
        BLOCK   53, 0
        ei
        ret
        BLOCK   198, 0

start_neoprogram:
        di
        im      1
        ; PG1 must be the physical page #03 before SP is moved into WIN1.
        ; At this point the bootstrap stack is still in WIN3 at #FFFE.
        ld      a, #03
        call    neo_set_pg1
        ld      sp, Z80_STACK_TOP     ; WIN1/page #03; DMA begins at #5000
        ; Fill the stack region with a canary before the first normal CALL/PUSH.
        ; The lower 128 bytes remain a guard; normal stack use grows down from #4FFE.
        ld      hl, Z80_STACK_GUARD_BASE
        ld      (hl), #A5
        ld      de, Z80_STACK_GUARD_BASE + 1
        ld      bc, Z80_STACK_FILL_LENGTH
        ldir

        ; Set Z80 to the documented maximum guaranteed clock: 20 MHz.
        ; Do not select 24 MHz: the guide explicitly says it is not guaranteed.
        in      a, (GSCFG0)
        and     #CF
        or      C_20MHZ
        out     (GSCFG0), a

        xor     a
        ld      (neo_dma_error), a
        ld      (saved_z80_ax), a
        ld      (saved_z80_ax+1), a
        ld      (saved_x86_ax), a
        ld      (saved_x86_ax+1), a
        ld      (x86_exit_status), a
        ld      (neo_command), a
        ld      (neo_tx_byte), a

        ; Copy the ROM-return trampoline into unoverlaid RAM at $4000.
        ld      hl, rom_return_trampoline_code
        ld      de, ROM_RETURN_TRAMPOLINE_ADDR
        ld      bc, rom_return_trampoline_end - rom_return_trampoline_code
        ldir

        ; Notify the Spectrum only after the new firmware is executing.
        ld      a, NGS_FIRMWARE_READY_ACK
        call    neo_send_data_byte

main_loop:
        ; A command exists only while CBIT (ZXSTAT bit 0) is set.
        in      a, (ZXSTAT)
        bit     B_CBIT, a
        jr      z, main_loop

        ; Read the command byte from ZXCMD. Reading it does NOT clear CBIT.
        in      a, (ZXCMD)
        ld      (neo_command), a

        ; Standard GS #F4 cold restart also returns this custom firmware to ROM.
        cp      GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART
        jp      z, command_return_to_gsrom
        cp      CMD_RESET
        jp      z, command_reset
        cp      CMD_LOAD_X86
        jp      z, command_load_x86
        cp      CMD_RUN_X86
        jp      z, command_run_x86
        cp      CMD_GET_STATUS
        jp      z, command_get_status
        cp      CMD_MEM_TEST
        jp      z, command_mem_test
        cp      CMD_BIOS_POST
        jp      z, command_bios_post
        cp      CMD_INT_TEST
        jp      z, command_int_test
        cp      CMD_INT_GUEST_TEST
        jp      z, command_int_guest_test
        cp      CMD_BIOS_VIDEO_TEST
        jp      z, command_bios_video_test
        cp      CMD_RUN_X86_REAL
        jp      z, command_run_x86_real

        ; Unknown command: release CBIT so the host cannot deadlock.
        xor     a
        out     (CLRCBIT), a
        jp      main_loop

; A #F4 command is also the standard GS105a cold-restart command.
; If our firmware is running, reveal the stock ROM again and jump to its
; reset vector. Leave CBIT asserted: the ROM's cold-start path clears it when
; the standard firmware is ready, so the host's long wait is a real handshake.
command_return_to_gsrom:
        ld      a, 3
        call    neo_set_pg1             ; restore trampoline's physical page
        jp      ROM_RETURN_TRAMPOLINE_ADDR

command_reset:
        ld      a, #03
        call    neo_set_pg1             ; ensure firmware page 1 is visible
        xor     a
        ld      (neo_dma_error), a
        ld      (x86_exit_status), a
        ld      (saved_z80_ax), a
        ld      (saved_z80_ax+1), a
        ld      (saved_x86_ax), a
        ld      (saved_x86_ax+1), a
        out     (CLRCBIT), a
        jp      main_loop

; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
; CMD_LOAD_X86
; 1) Keep default WIN1 page #03 selected; logical $5000 maps to $00D000.
; 2) Select ZX DMA module and set physical DMA target $00D000.
; 3) Enable DMA by setting DMA_CST bit 7.
; 4) Clear CBIT: this is the READY indication to the host.
; 5) Wait for host's end marker (#FF written to GSCOM, setting CBIT).
; 6) Disable DMA, then clear CBIT to acknowledge completion.
;
; Host uses OUT/LDIR writes into $0000-$3FFF. No dummy write is performed.
; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
command_load_x86:
        ; Keep the documented default WIN1 page selected. DMA writes to
        ; physical $00D000, which appears at logical $5000 in page #03.
        ld      a, #03
        call    neo_set_pg1

        ld      a, C_DMA_ZX
        out     (DMA_MOD), a

        xor     a
        out     (DMA_HAD), a            ; physical address bits 21:16 = 0
        ld      a, #D0
        out     (DMA_MAD), a            ; physical address bits 15:8 = #D0
        xor     a
        out     (DMA_LAD), a            ; physical address bits 7:0 = 0

        ld      a, #80
        out     (DMA_CST), a            ; DMA enable = bit 7

        xor     a
        out     (CLRCBIT), a            ; host may now start LDIR

        ld      d, DMA_WAIT_OUTER
.dma_wait_outer:
        ld      bc, DMA_WAIT_LIMIT
.dma_wait_host_end:
        in      a, (ZXSTAT)
        bit     B_CBIT, a
        jr      nz, .dma_host_end
        dec     bc
        ld      a, b
        or      c
        jr      nz, .dma_wait_host_end
        dec     d
        jr      nz, .dma_wait_outer

        ; Timeout: close DMA, restore PG1 and release the host command line.
        xor     a
        out     (DMA_CST), a
        out     (CLRCBIT), a
        ld      a, #03
        call    neo_set_pg1             ; restore page 1 on DMA timeout
        ld      a, 1
        ld      (neo_dma_error), a
        ld      a, 3
        ld      (x86_exit_status), a
        jp      main_loop

.dma_host_end:
        xor     a
        out     (DMA_CST), a            ; disable DMA before leaving handler
        out     (CLRCBIT), a            ; acknowledge host's #FF marker
        xor     a
        ld      (neo_dma_error), a
        jp      main_loop
neogs_dma_handler_end:

; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
; CMD_RUN_X86 — execute the length-framed payload beginning at $5002.
; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
command_run_x86:
        call    execute_neogs_x86
        ld      a, #03
        call    neo_set_pg1             ; restore firmware/trampoline page
        xor     a
        out     (CLRCBIT), a
        jp      main_loop

execute_neogs_x86:
        ld      a, (neo_dma_error)
        or      a
        jr      z, .dma_ok
        ld      hl, $FFFF
        ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
        ld      a, 3
        ld      (x86_exit_status), a
        ret

.dma_ok:
        ; Emit a live NeoGS-side dump before interpreting the DMA payload.
        ; The host drains this stream and mirrors it to emulator port #EF.
        call    neo_debug_dump_dma_frame

        ; DMA frame begins with a little-endian x86 byte length at $5000.
        ld      hl, (X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR)
        ld      a, h
        or      l
        jp      z, .invalid_x86_length

        ; Maximum code length is $2FFE: $5002 + length must not exceed $8000.
        ld      a, h
        cp      #2F
        jr      c, .x86_length_valid
        jp      nz, .invalid_x86_length
        ld      a, l
        cp      #FF
        jp      nc, .invalid_x86_length

.x86_length_valid:
        ld      de, X86_RAM_RUN_ADDR
        add     hl, de
        ld      (x86_program_end), hl

        ld      hl, 0
        ld      (saved_z80_ax), hl
        ld      hl, #FFFF
        ld      (x86_steps_remaining), hl
        xor     a
        ld      (x86_exit_status), a

        ld      bc, X86_RAM_RUN_ADDR
        call    x86_execute_loop

        ld      hl, (saved_z80_ax)
        ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
        ret

.invalid_x86_length:
        ld      hl, #FFFF
        ld      (saved_z80_ax), hl
        ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
        ld      a, 4
        ld      (x86_exit_status), a
        ret

; Execute the loaded DMA task as bounded guest code at 0000:5002.
; The DMA frame is copied into the x86 physical-memory backend before execution.
execute_neogs_x86_real:
        ; Match the legacy loader's DMA error and length validation.
        ld      a, (neo_dma_error)
        or      a
        jr      z, .real_dma_ok
        ld      hl, #FFFF
        ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
        ld      a, 3
        ld      (x86_exit_status), a
        scf
        ret

.real_dma_ok:
        ld      hl, (X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR)
        ld      a, h
        or      l
        jp      z, .real_invalid_length
        ld      a, h
        cp      #2F
        jr      c, .real_length_valid
        jp      nz, .real_invalid_length
        ld      a, l
        cp      #FF
        jp      nc, .real_invalid_length

.real_length_valid:
        ld      de, X86_RAM_RUN_ADDR
        add     hl, de
        ld      (x86_real_task_end), hl

        call    x86_mem_init
        ld      hl, X86_RAM_RUN_ADDR
        ld      de, #5002
        ld      bc, (X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR)
        call    x86_real_copy_image
        jp      c, .real_copy_failed

        ; Reset the guest register file to a deterministic real-mode entry.
        ld      hl, 0
        ld      (x86_reg_ax), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_bx), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_cx), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_dx), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_bp), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_si), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_di), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_es), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_ds), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_cs), hl
        ld      hl, #5002
        ld      (x86_reg_ip), hl
        ld      hl, #7000
        ld      (x86_reg_ss), hl
        ld      hl, #0100
        ld      (x86_reg_sp), hl
        ld      hl, #0202
        ld      (x86_reg_flags), hl

        ld      a, 1
        ld      (x86_real_bounds_enable), a
        call    x86_real_execute
        push    af
        ld      hl, (x86_reg_ax)
        ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
        xor     a
        ld      (x86_real_bounds_enable), a
        pop     af
        ret

.real_invalid_length:
        ld      hl, #FFFF
        ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
        ld      a, 4
        ld      (x86_exit_status), a
        scf
        ret

.real_copy_failed:
        ld      hl, #1D01
        ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
        ld      a, 4
        ld      (x86_exit_status), a
        scf
        ret

; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
; CMD_MEM_TEST — destructive test of the reserved 1 MiB x86 RAM region.
; Result is returned through the existing CMD_GET_STATUS frame.
; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
command_mem_test:
        call    x86_mem_test
        xor     a
        out     (CLRCBIT), a
        jp      main_loop

; BIOS low-memory foundation POST.
command_bios_post:
        call    bios_post
        xor     a
        out     (CLRCBIT), a
        jp      main_loop

; Stage 2A: validate 8086 INT/IRET stack-frame semantics.
command_int_test:
        call    x86_int_self_test
        xor     a
        out     (CLRCBIT), a
        jp      main_loop

; Stage 2B: execute a guest INT handler through CS:IP and return with IRET.
command_int_guest_test:
        call    x86_real_mode_test
        xor     a
        out     (CLRCBIT), a
        jp      main_loop

; Stage 3A: guest INT 10h video BIOS service test.
command_bios_video_test:
        call    bios_int10_video_test
        xor     a
        out     (CLRCBIT), a
        jp      main_loop

; Run a TR-DOS payload through the bounded CS:IP real-mode executor.
command_run_x86_real:
        call    execute_neogs_x86_real
        ; The guard is checked after all earlier diagnostics and the real-mode task.
        call    z80_stack_guard_check
        jr      nc, .stack_guard_ok
        ld      hl, #7E00
        ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
        ld      a, 12
        ld      (x86_exit_status), a
.stack_guard_ok:
        ld      a, 3
        call    neo_set_pg1             ; restore physical firmware page 1
        xor     a
        out     (CLRCBIT), a
        jp      main_loop

; Check the lower 128-byte canary in WIN1/page #03.
; Carry set means the Z80 stack crossed the #4880 safety boundary.
z80_stack_guard_check:
        ld      hl, Z80_STACK_GUARD_BASE
        ld      b, Z80_STACK_GUARD_SIZE
.loop:
        ld      a, (hl)
        cp      #A5
        jr      nz, .fail
        inc     hl
        djnz    .loop
        or      a
        ret
.fail:
        scf
        ret

; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
; CMD_GET_STATUS — application-level frame over the documented GSDAT stream:
;   A5 signature, exit status, AX low, AX high.
; Every byte uses DBIT: write ZXDATWR -> host reads GSDAT -> DBIT clears.
; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
command_get_status:
        ld      a, NGS_RESULT_SIGNATURE
        call    neo_send_data_byte
        jr      c, .status_tx_failed

        ld      a, (x86_exit_status)
        call    neo_send_data_byte
        jr      c, .status_tx_failed

        ld      hl, (saved_x86_ax)
        ld      a, l
        call    neo_send_data_byte
        jr      c, .status_tx_failed

        ld      a, h
        call    neo_send_data_byte
        jr      c, .status_tx_failed

        xor     a
        out     (CLRCBIT), a
        jp      main_loop

.status_tx_failed:
        xor     a
        out     (CLRCBIT), a
        jp      main_loop

; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
; NeoGS DMA diagnostic stream.
; Sent through GSDAT/DBIT while the host is in
; neogs_wait_run_done_with_console; the host mirrors it to port #EF.
; Dumps PG1, the two-byte length header, and the first 12 frame bytes.
; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
neo_debug_dump_dma_frame:
        push    hl
        push    de
        ld      hl, neo_debug_dma_prefix
        call    neo_debug_send_string
        ld      hl, X86_DMA_HEADER_ADDR
        ld      e, 12
.neo_debug_dump_loop:
        ld      a, (hl)
        call    neo_debug_hex8
        ld      a, ' '
        call    neo_send_data_byte
        inc     hl
        dec     e
        jr      nz, .neo_debug_dump_loop
        ld      a, 13
        call    neo_send_data_byte
        ld      a, 10
        call    neo_send_data_byte
        pop     de
        pop     hl
        ret

neo_debug_send_string:
        ld      a, (hl)
        inc     hl
        or      a
        ret     z
        call    neo_send_data_byte
        jr      neo_debug_send_string

; IN: A = byte. Sends two uppercase hexadecimal digits.
neo_debug_hex8:
        push    af
        rrca
        rrca
        rrca
        rrca
        and     #0F
        call    neo_debug_hex_nibble
        pop     af
        and     #0F
        jp      neo_debug_hex_nibble

; IN: A = nibble 0..15.
neo_debug_hex_nibble:
        add     a, '0'
        cp      '9'+1
        jr      c, .neo_debug_hex_send
        add     a, 7
.neo_debug_hex_send:
        jp      neo_send_data_byte

neo_debug_dma_prefix:
        defb    "[NGS DMA] FRAME=", 0

; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
; neo_send_data_byte
; IN: A = byte to send to Spectrum.
; Wait for DBIT=0, write ZXDATWR (#03), then wait for host read to clear DBIT.
; OUT: carry clear on success, carry set on timeout. Preserves HL and DE.
; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
neo_send_data_byte:
        ld      (neo_tx_byte), a
        ld      bc, IPC_WAIT_LIMIT
.tx_wait_slot:
        in      a, (ZXSTAT)
        bit     B_DBIT, a
        jr      z, .tx_slot_free
        dec     bc
        ld      a, b
        or      c
        jr      nz, .tx_wait_slot
        scf
        ret

.tx_slot_free:
        ld      a, (neo_tx_byte)
        out     (ZXDATWR), a

        ld      bc, IPC_WAIT_LIMIT
.tx_wait_host_read:
        in      a, (ZXSTAT)
        bit     B_DBIT, a
        jr      z, .tx_host_read
        dec     bc
        ld      a, b
        or      c
        jr      nz, .tx_wait_host_read
        scf
        ret

.tx_host_read:
        or      a
        ret

; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
; Select the physical 16 KiB page visible in NeoGS logical window 1.
; WIN1 is the GS-side port #21 from the official ports.inc interface.
; Input: A = physical 16 KiB page number.
; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
neo_set_pg1:
        out     (PG1), a
        ret

neo_command:       db 0
neo_tx_byte:       db 0
neo_dma_error:     db 0
saved_z80_ax:      dw 0
saved_x86_ax:      dw 0

; This code is copied to $4000 during startup. Its OUT changes the ROM map,
; but the following JP is still fetched from RAM because PC remains in $4000.
rom_return_trampoline_code:
        in      a, (GSCFG0)
        and     #F6                    ; clear M_NOROM and M_EXPAG
        out     (GSCFG0), a
        xor     a
        out     (MPAG), a                ; restore stock ROM paging: WIN2=ROM0, WIN3=ROM1
        jp      0
rom_return_trampoline_end:

        INCLUDE "x86_memory.asm"
        INCLUDE "bios/bios_core.asm"
        INCLUDE "x86_modrm.asm"
        INCLUDE "x86_interpreter.asm"
        INCLUDE "x86_interrupt.asm"
        INCLUDE "x86_real_mode.asm"
        INCLUDE "x86_real_alu.asm"
        INCLUDE "x86_real_alu8.asm"
        INCLUDE "x86_real_mov.asm"
        INCLUDE "x86_io.asm"
        INCLUDE "x86_strings.asm"
        INCLUDE "bios/bios_video.asm"

; WIN1 layout: trampoline $4000-$400F, guard $4800-$487F, stack grows
