x86_mov_disp_low:      db 0

; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
; Extended 8086 MOV addressing regression.
; Covers all eight 16-bit EA forms, signed disp8, disp16, ES/CS/SS/DS
; overrides, BP default-SS behavior, and a word store at offset FFFFh.
; Guest code executes at CS:IP = 0800:5600 (physical D600h).
; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
x86_real_mov_addressing_test:
        ld      hl, x86_mov_address_test_image
        ld      de, #D600
        ld      bc, x86_mov_address_test_image_end - x86_mov_address_test_image
        call    x86_real_copy_image
        jp      c, .fail_copy

        ld      hl, 0
        ld      (x86_reg_ax), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_bx), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_cx), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_dx), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_bp), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_si), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_di), hl
        ld      hl, #1000
        ld      (x86_reg_ds), hl
        ld      hl, #3000
        ld      (x86_reg_es), hl
        ld      hl, #0800
        ld      (x86_reg_cs), hl
        ld      hl, #7000
        ld      (x86_reg_ss), hl
        ld      hl, #5600
        ld      (x86_reg_ip), hl
        ld      hl, #0100
        ld      (x86_reg_sp), hl
        ld      hl, #0202
        ld      (x86_reg_flags), hl
        xor     a
        ld      (x86_real_seg_override_active), a

        call    x86_real_execute
        jp      c, .fail_execute

        ; Table records are segment, offset, expected word (little-endian).
        ld      hl, x86_mov_address_test_table
        ld      (x86_mov_address_test_ptr), hl
        ld      a, x86_mov_address_test_count
        ld      (x86_mov_address_test_remaining), a
        xor     a
        ld      (x86_mov_address_test_index), a
.verify_loop:
        ld      hl, (x86_mov_address_test_ptr)
        ld      e, (hl)
        inc     hl
        ld      d, (hl)
        inc     hl
        ld      (x86_mem_segment), de
        ld      e, (hl)
        inc     hl
        ld      d, (hl)
        inc     hl
        ld      (x86_mem_offset), de
        ld      (x86_mov_address_test_ptr), hl
        call    x86_mem_read16
        jp      c, .fail_verify
        ld      (x86_mov_address_test_actual), hl
        ld      hl, (x86_mov_address_test_ptr)
        ld      e, (hl)
        inc     hl
        ld      d, (hl)
        inc     hl
        ld      (x86_mov_address_test_ptr), hl
        ld      hl, (x86_mov_address_test_actual)
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      nz, .fail_verify
        ld      a, (x86_mov_address_test_index)
        inc     a
        ld      (x86_mov_address_test_index), a
        ld      a, (x86_mov_address_test_remaining)
        dec     a
        ld      (x86_mov_address_test_remaining), a
        jp      nz, .verify_loop

        ld      hl, #1E01
        ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
        xor     a
        ld      (x86_exit_status), a
        or      a
        ret

.fail_copy:
        ld      hl, #1E10
        jp      .fail
.fail_execute:
        ld      hl, #1E11
        jp      .fail
.fail_verify:
        ld      a, (x86_mov_address_test_index)
        ld      l, a
        ld      h, #1E
        jp      .fail
.fail:
        ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
        ld      a, 11
        ld      (x86_exit_status), a
        scf
        ret

x86_mov_address_test_remaining: db 0
x86_mov_address_test_index:     db 0
x86_mov_address_test_ptr:       dw 0
x86_mov_address_test_actual:    dw 0

x86_mov_address_test_image:
        db #B8,#34,#12             ; MOV AX,1234
        db #BB,#00,#02             ; MOV BX,0200
        db #BE,#10,#00             ; MOV SI,0010
        db #BF,#20,#00             ; MOV DI,0020
        db #BD,#00,#01             ; MOV BP,0100

        db #89,#00                 ; [BX+SI] -> DS:0210
        db #89,#01                 ; [BX+DI] -> DS:0220
        db #89,#02                 ; [BP+SI] -> SS:0110
        db #89,#03                 ; [BP+DI] -> SS:0120
        db #89,#04                 ; [SI] -> DS:0010
        db #89,#05                 ; [DI] -> DS:0020
        db #89,#46,#00             ; [BP+0] -> SS:0100
        db #89,#07                 ; [BX] -> DS:0200

        db #89,#40,#05             ; [BX+SI+5] positive disp8 -> DS:0215
        db #89,#40,#FB             ; [BX+SI-5] negative disp8 -> DS:020B
        db #89,#80,#34,#12         ; [BX+SI+1234] disp16 -> DS:1444

        db #26,#89,#07             ; ES:[BX] -> ES:0200
        db #2E,#89,#07             ; CS:[BX] -> CS:0200
        db #36,#89,#07             ; SS:[BX] -> SS:0200
        db #3E,#89,#46,#00         ; DS:[BP] -> DS:0100

        ; Read back every EA form and preserve each result in DS scratch.
        db #8B,#08                 ; CX <- [BX+SI]
        db #89,#0E,#00,#05         ; DS:0500 <- CX
        db #8B,#09                 ; CX <- [BX+DI]
        db #89,#0E,#02,#05         ; DS:0502 <- CX
        db #8B,#0A                 ; CX <- [BP+SI]
        db #89,#0E,#04,#05         ; DS:0504 <- CX
        db #8B,#0B                 ; CX <- [BP+DI]
        db #89,#0E,#06,#05         ; DS:0506 <- CX
        db #8B,#0C                 ; CX <- [SI]
        db #89,#0E,#08,#05         ; DS:0508 <- CX
        db #8B,#0D                 ; CX <- [DI]
        db #89,#0E,#0A,#05         ; DS:050A <- CX
        db #8B,#4E,#00             ; CX <- [BP+0]
        db #89,#0E,#0C,#05         ; DS:050C <- CX
        db #8B,#0F                 ; CX <- [BX]
        db #89,#0E,#0E,#05         ; DS:050E <- CX

        db #26,#8B,#0F             ; CX <- ES:[BX]
        db #89,#0E,#10,#05         ; DS:0510 <- CX
        db #2E,#8B,#0F             ; CX <- CS:[BX]
        db #89,#0E,#12,#05         ; DS:0512 <- CX
        db #36,#8B,#0F             ; CX <- SS:[BX]
        db #89,#0E,#14,#05         ; DS:0514 <- CX
        db #3E,#8B,#4E,#00         ; CX <- DS:[BP]
        db #89,#0E,#16,#05         ; DS:0516 <- CX

        db #8B,#48,#05             ; CX <- [BX+SI+5]
        db #89,#0E,#20,#05         ; DS:0520 <- CX
        db #8B,#48,#FB             ; CX <- [BX+SI-5]
        db #89,#0E,#22,#05         ; DS:0522 <- CX
        db #8B,#88,#34,#12         ; CX <- [BX+SI+1234]
        db #89,#0E,#24,#05         ; DS:0524 <- CX

        db #BB,#FF,#FF             ; BX=FFFF
        db #89,#07                 ; word at DS:FFFF wraps offset to 0000
        db #8B,#0F                 ; CX <- [BX], same segment offset wrap
        db #89,#0E,#26,#05         ; DS:0526 <- CX
        db #F4
x86_mov_address_test_image_end:

; segment, offset, expected word
x86_mov_address_test_table:
        dw #1000,#0210,#1234       ; [BX+SI]
        dw #1000,#0220,#1234       ; [BX+DI]
        dw #7000,#0110,#1234       ; [BP+SI], default SS
        dw #7000,#0120,#1234       ; [BP+DI], default SS
        dw #1000,#0010,#1234       ; [SI]
        dw #1000,#0020,#1234       ; [DI]
        dw #7000,#0100,#1234       ; [BP], default SS
        dw #1000,#0200,#1234       ; [BX]
        dw #1000,#0215,#1234       ; positive disp8
        dw #1000,#020B,#1234       ; negative disp8
        dw #1000,#1444,#1234       ; disp16
        dw #3000,#0200,#1234       ; ES override on DS-default [BX]
        dw #0800,#0200,#1234       ; CS override on DS-default [BX]
        dw #7000,#0200,#1234       ; SS override on DS-default [BX]
        dw #1000,#0100,#1234       ; DS override on SS-default [BP]
        dw #1000,#FFFF,#1234       ; word wraps within same segment
        dw #1000,#0500,#1234       ; read [BX+SI]
        dw #1000,#0502,#1234       ; read [BX+DI]
        dw #1000,#0504,#1234       ; read [BP+SI]
        dw #1000,#0506,#1234       ; read [BP+DI]
        dw #1000,#0508,#1234       ; read [SI]
        dw #1000,#050A,#1234       ; read [DI]
        dw #1000,#050C,#1234       ; read [BP]
        dw #1000,#050E,#1234       ; read [BX]
        dw #1000,#0510,#1234       ; read ES override
        dw #1000,#0512,#1234       ; read CS override
        dw #1000,#0514,#1234       ; read SS override
        dw #1000,#0516,#1234       ; read DS override
        dw #1000,#0520,#1234       ; read positive disp8
        dw #1000,#0522,#1234       ; read negative disp8
        dw #1000,#0524,#1234       ; read disp16
        dw #1000,#0526,#1234       ; read wrapped word at DS:FFFF
x86_mov_address_test_count EQU 32

; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
; Regression test for all four MOV ModR/M opcodes and DS/SS effective
; addressing. Success: carry clear, saved_x86_ax=1D01h.
; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
x86_real_mov_memory_test:
        ld      hl, x86_mov_memory_test_image
        ld      de, #5600
        ld      bc, x86_mov_memory_test_image_end - x86_mov_memory_test_image
        call    x86_real_copy_image
        jr      nc, .image_ready
        ld      hl, #1D01
        jp      .fail

.image_ready:
        ld      hl, 0
        ld      (x86_reg_ax), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_bx), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_cx), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_dx), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_bp), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_si), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_di), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_ds), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_es), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_cs), hl
        ; ld      a, 1
        ; ld      (x86_mov_trace_enable), a
        ; ld      hl, #FFFF
        ; ld      (x86_mov_diag_value), hl
        ; ld      hl, #FFFF
        ; ld      (x86_mov_diag_offset), hl
        ld      hl, #5600
        ld      (x86_reg_ip), hl
        ld      hl, #7000
        ld      (x86_reg_ss), hl
        ld      hl, #0100
        ld      (x86_reg_sp), hl
        ld      hl, #0202
        ld      (x86_reg_flags), hl

        call    x86_real_execute
        ; xor     a
        ; ld      (x86_mov_trace_enable), a
        jr      nc, .execution_ok
        ld      hl, #1D02
        jp      .fail

.execution_ok:
        ld      hl, (x86_reg_ax)
        ld      de, #1234
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      nz, .fail_ax
        ld      hl, (x86_reg_cx)
        ld      de, #1234
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      nz, .fail_cx
        ld      hl, (x86_reg_dx)
        ld      de, #AB34
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      nz, .fail_dx
        ld      hl, (x86_reg_bx)
        ld      de, #AB34
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      nz, .fail_bx
        ld      hl, (x86_reg_di)
        ld      de, #AB34
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      nz, .fail_di
        ld      hl, (x86_reg_si)
        ld      de, #1234
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      nz, .fail_si

        ; Verify each memory destination independently. Distinct failure
        ; codes identify the exact location without changing test semantics.
        ld      hl, 0
        ld      (x86_mem_segment), hl
        ld      hl, #0200
        ld      (x86_mem_offset), hl
        call    x86_mem_read16
        jp      c, .fail_mem_0
        ld      de, #1234
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      nz, .fail_mem_0

        ld      hl, 0
        ld      (x86_mem_segment), hl
        ld      hl, #0000
        ld      (x86_mem_offset), hl
        call    x86_mem_read16
        jp      c, .fail_mem_1
        ld      de, #AB34
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      nz, .fail_mem_1

        ld      hl, 0
        ld      (x86_mem_segment), hl
        ld      hl, #0206
        ld      (x86_mem_offset), hl
        call    x86_mem_read16
        jp      c, .fail_mem_2
        ld      de, #AB34
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      nz, .fail_mem_2

        ; BP-based forms must default to SS rather than DS.
        ld      hl, #7000
        ld      (x86_mem_segment), hl
        ld      hl, #0100
        ld      (x86_mem_offset), hl
        call    x86_mem_read16
        jp      c, .fail_mem_3
        ld      de, #1234
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      nz, .fail_mem_3

        ld      hl, #1D01
        ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
        xor     a
        ld      (x86_exit_status), a
        or      a
        ret

.fail_ax:
        ld      hl, #1D30
        jp      .fail
.fail_cx:
        ld      hl, #1D31
        jp      .fail
.fail_dx:
        ld      hl, #1D32
        jp      .fail
.fail_bx:
        ld      hl, #1D33
        jp      .fail
.fail_di:
        ld      hl, #1D34
        jp      .fail
.fail_si:
        ld      hl, #1D35
        jp      .fail
.fail_mem_0:
        ld      hl, #1D40
        jp      .fail
.fail_mem_1:
        ld      hl, #1D41
        jp      .fail
.fail_mem_2:
        ld      hl, #1D42
        jp      .fail
.fail_mem_3:
        ld      hl, #1D43
        jp      .fail
.fail_memory:
        ld      hl, #1D04
.fail:
        ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
        ld      a, 11
        ld      (x86_exit_status), a
        scf
        ret

x86_mov_memory_test_image:
        db #B8,#34,#12             ; MOV AX,1234
        db #8B,#C8                 ; MOV CX,AX (ModR/M register-direct)
        db #89,#CA                 ; MOV DX,CX (reverse register-direct)
        db #BB,#00,#02             ; MOV BX,0200
        db #89,#07                 ; MOV [BX],AX
        db #8B,#0F                 ; MOV CX,[BX]
        db #88,#4F,#02             ; MOV [BX+02],CL
        db #8A,#57,#02             ; MOV DL,[BX+02]
        db #BA,#34,#AB             ; MOV DX,AB34
        db #89,#14                 ; MOV [SI],DX (SI initially zero)
        db #8B,#1C                 ; MOV BX,[SI]
        db #89,#16,#06,#02         ; MOV [0206],DX
        db #8B,#3E,#06,#02         ; MOV DI,[0206]
        db #BD,#00,#01             ; MOV BP,0100
        db #89,#46,#00             ; MOV [BP+00],AX (SS default)
        db #8B,#76,#00             ; MOV SI,[BP+00] (SS default)
        db #F4
x86_mov_memory_test_image_end:

; ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
; ; MOV trace to hardware debug port #EF.
; ; Emits stage, opcode, ModR/M, decoded segment:offset, source value,
; ; DX/SI/DI, and immediate post-store readback. Enabled only by MOV self-test.
; ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
; x86_mov_trace_event:
;         push    af
;         push    bc
;         push    de
;         push    hl
;         ld      (x86_mov_trace_stage), a
;         cp      'D'
;         jr      nz, .trace_keep_readback
;         ld      hl, #FFFF
;         ld      (x86_mov_trace_readback), hl
; .trace_keep_readback:
;         ld      a, (x86_mov_trace_enable)
;         or      a
;         jp      z, .trace_done
;
;         ld      a, 'M'
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;         ld      a, (x86_mov_trace_stage)
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;         ld      a, ' '
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;
;         ld      a, 'I'
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;         ld      a, 'P'
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;         ld      a, '='
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;         ld      hl, (x86_reg_ip)
;         call    x86_mov_trace_hex16
;         ld      a, ' '
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;
;         ld      a, 'O'
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;         ld      a, 'P'
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;         ld      a, '='
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;         ld      a, (x86_mov_opcode)
;         call    x86_mov_trace_hex8
;         ld      a, '/'
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;         ld      a, (x86_mov_modrm)
;         call    x86_mov_trace_hex8
;         ld      a, ' '
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;
;         ld      a, 'E'
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;         ld      a, 'A'
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;         ld      a, '='
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;         ld      hl, (x86_mov_segment)
;         call    x86_mov_trace_hex16
;         ld      a, ':'
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;         ld      hl, (x86_mov_offset)
;         call    x86_mov_trace_hex16
;         ld      a, ' '
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;
;         ld      a, 'V'
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;         ld      a, '='
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;         ld      hl, (x86_mov_value)
;         call    x86_mov_trace_hex16
;         ld      a, ' '
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;
;         ld      a, 'R'
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;         ld      a, '='
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;         ld      hl, (x86_reg_ax)
;         call    x86_mov_trace_hex16
;         ld      a, '/'
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;         ld      hl, (x86_reg_bx)
;         call    x86_mov_trace_hex16
;         ld      a, '/'
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;         ld      hl, (x86_reg_cx)
;         call    x86_mov_trace_hex16
;         ld      a, '/'
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;         ld      hl, (x86_reg_dx)
;         call    x86_mov_trace_hex16
;         ld      a, '/'
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;         ld      hl, (x86_reg_si)
;         call    x86_mov_trace_hex16
;         ld      a, '/'
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;         ld      hl, (x86_reg_di)
;         call    x86_mov_trace_hex16
;         ld      a, ' '
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;
;         ld      a, 'B'
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;         ld      a, '='
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;         ld      hl, (x86_mov_trace_readback)
;         call    x86_mov_trace_hex16
;         ld      a, 13
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;         ld      a, 10
;         call    x86_mov_trace_putc
;
; .trace_done:
;         pop     hl
;         pop     de
;         pop     bc
;         pop     af
;         ret
;
; x86_mov_trace_putc:
;         ; NeoGS local I/O is not the Spectrum printer port. Send each
;         ; trace byte through the existing DBIT stream; the host drains it
;         ; while CMD_INT_GUEST_TEST is active and mirrors it to port #EF.
;         push    bc
;         push    de
;         push    hl
;         call    neo_send_data_byte
;         pop     hl
;         pop     de
;         pop     bc
;         ret
;
; ; IN A=byte.
; x86_mov_trace_hex8:
;         push    af
;         rra
;         rra
;         rra
;         rra
;         call    x86_mov_trace_nibble
;         pop     af
;         jp      x86_mov_trace_nibble
;
; x86_mov_trace_nibble:
;         and     #0F
;         cp      10
;         jr      c, .trace_digit
;         add     a, 7
; .trace_digit:
;         add     a, '0'
;         jp      x86_mov_trace_putc
;
; ; IN HL=word.
; x86_mov_trace_hex16:
;         ld      a, h
;         call    x86_mov_trace_hex8
;         ld      a, l
;         jp      x86_mov_trace_hex8
;
;

; =========================================================================
; FILE: neogs_sub/bios/bios_core.asm
; =========================================================================
; =========================================================================
; PROJECT 124 — BIOS foundation / POST, stage 1
;
; Establishes the guest-visible low-memory data layout for a future 8086
; real-mode BIOS. This stage does not implement executable BIOS ROM or INT
; services. IVT is initialized to zero; vectors are installed only after the
; CPU INT/IRET path and guest stack semantics are implemented.
;
; Physical memory profile:
;   00000-003FF  IVT (256 entries, 4 bytes each)
;   00400-004FF  BIOS Data Area (BDA)
;   00500-9FFFF  conventional RAM profile (640 KiB)
;   A0000-BFFFF  reserved video memory
;   C0000-EFFFF  reserved option-ROM/device window
;   F0000-FFFFF  reserved P124 BIOS ROM (not read-only yet)
;
; Entry: bios_post
; Success: x86_exit_status=0, saved_x86_ax=#B105
; Failure: x86_exit_status=7, saved_x86_ax=#B1xx
; No DMA/IPC transport changes.
; =========================================================================

BIOS_IVT_BASE             EQU #00000
BIOS_IVT_SIZE             EQU #00400
BIOS_BDA_BASE             EQU #00400
BIOS_BDA_SIZE             EQU #00100
BIOS_LOW_MEMORY_END       EQU #00500
BIOS_CONVENTIONAL_KB      EQU 640
BIOS_VIDEO_MODE_TEXT      EQU #03
BIOS_TEXT_COLUMNS         EQU 80
BIOS_TEXT_ROWS            EQU 25
BIOS_POST_SIGNATURE       EQU #B105

bios_post:
        call    x86_mem_init
        call    bios_clear_ivt_bda
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_1

        ; Equipment word: no legacy IBM-PC equipment bits are asserted.
        ld      hl, #0410
        xor     a
        call    bios_write_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_2
        ld      hl, #0411
        xor     a
        call    bios_write_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_2

        ; Conventional memory size = 640 KiB.
        ld      hl, #0413
        ld      a, #80
        call    bios_write_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_3
        ld      hl, #0414
        ld      a, #02
        call    bios_write_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_3

        ; Empty keyboard ring buffer. Head and tail point to offset 001Eh.
        ld      hl, #041A
        ld      a, #1E
        call    bios_write_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_4
        ld      hl, #041B
        xor     a
        call    bios_write_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_4
        ld      hl, #041C
        ld      a, #1E
        call    bios_write_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_4
        ld      hl, #041D
        xor     a
        call    bios_write_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_4

        ; Text mode 03h, 80 columns, 4 KiB page, page zero active.
        ld      hl, #0449
        ld      a, BIOS_VIDEO_MODE_TEXT
        call    bios_write_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_5
        ld      hl, #044A
        ld      a, BIOS_TEXT_COLUMNS
        call    bios_write_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_5
        ld      hl, #044B
        xor     a
        call    bios_write_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_5
        ld      hl, #044C
        xor     a
        call    bios_write_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_5
        ld      hl, #044D
        ld      a, #10
        call    bios_write_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_5
        ld      hl, #0462
        xor     a
        call    bios_write_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_5

        ; 25 rows are represented as rows-minus-one; character height = 16.
        ld      hl, #0484
        ld      a, BIOS_TEXT_ROWS - 1
        call    bios_write_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_6
        ld      hl, #0485
        ld      a, #10
        call    bios_write_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_6
        ld      hl, #0486
        xor     a
        call    bios_write_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_6

        ; Read back every IVT byte, then verify the published BDA profile.
        call    bios_verify_ivt
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_7

        ld      hl, #0410
        xor     a
        call    bios_expect_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_8
        ld      hl, #0411
        xor     a
        call    bios_expect_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_8
        ld      hl, #0413
        ld      a, #80
        call    bios_expect_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_8
        ld      hl, #0414
        ld      a, #02
        call    bios_expect_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_8
        ld      hl, #041A
        ld      a, #1E
        call    bios_expect_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_8
        ld      hl, #041B
        xor     a
        call    bios_expect_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_8
        ld      hl, #041C
        ld      a, #1E
        call    bios_expect_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_8
        ld      hl, #041D
        xor     a
        call    bios_expect_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_8
        ld      hl, #0449
        ld      a, BIOS_VIDEO_MODE_TEXT
        call    bios_expect_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_8
        ld      hl, #044A
        ld      a, BIOS_TEXT_COLUMNS
        call    bios_expect_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_8
        ld      hl, #044B
        xor     a
        call    bios_expect_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_8
        ld      hl, #044C
        xor     a
        call    bios_expect_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_8
        ld      hl, #044D
        ld      a, #10
        call    bios_expect_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_8
        ld      hl, #0462
        xor     a
        call    bios_expect_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_8
        ld      hl, #0484
        ld      a, BIOS_TEXT_ROWS - 1
        call    bios_expect_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_8
        ld      hl, #0485
        ld      a, #10
        call    bios_expect_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_8
        ld      hl, #0486
        xor     a
        call    bios_expect_byte
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_8

        ; Publish the INT 10h firmware trap after the IVT zero-fill check.
        call    bios_int10_install_vector
        jp      c, bios_post_fail_8

        ld      hl, BIOS_POST_SIGNATURE
        ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
        xor     a
        ld      (x86_exit_status), a
        or      a
        ret

; Clear IVT + BDA (first 1280 bytes).
bios_clear_ivt_bda:
        ld      hl, BIOS_IVT_BASE
        ld      (bios_clear_address), hl
        ld      hl, BIOS_LOW_MEMORY_END
        ld      (bios_clear_remaining), hl
        xor     a
        ld      (x86_mem_value), a

.bios_clear_loop:
        ld      hl, (bios_clear_address)
        ld      (x86_mem_addr), hl
        xor     a
        ld      (x86_mem_addr+2), a
        call    x86_mem_write8
        ret     c
        ld      hl, (bios_clear_address)
        inc     hl
        ld      (bios_clear_address), hl
        ld      hl, (bios_clear_remaining)
        dec     hl
        ld      (bios_clear_remaining), hl
        ld      a, h
        or      l
        jr      nz, .bios_clear_loop
        or      a
        ret

; Verify all 1024 bytes of the IVT are zero.
bios_verify_ivt:
        ld      hl, BIOS_IVT_BASE
        ld      (bios_verify_address), hl
        ld      hl, BIOS_IVT_SIZE
        ld      (bios_verify_remaining), hl
.bios_verify_loop:
        ld      hl, (bios_verify_address)
        ld      (x86_mem_addr), hl
        xor     a
        ld      (x86_mem_addr+2), a
        call    x86_mem_read8
        ret     c
        or      a
        jr      nz, .bios_verify_failed
        ld      hl, (bios_verify_address)
        inc     hl
        ld      (bios_verify_address), hl
        ld      hl, (bios_verify_remaining)
        dec     hl
        ld      (bios_verify_remaining), hl
        ld      a, h
        or      l
        jr      nz, .bios_verify_loop
        or      a
        ret
.bios_verify_failed:
        scf
        ret

; IN: HL=16-bit physical address, A=value. Carry on memory error.
bios_write_byte:
        ld      (bios_work_value), a
        ld      (x86_mem_addr), hl
        xor     a
        ld      (x86_mem_addr+2), a
        ld      a, (bios_work_value)
        ld      (x86_mem_value), a
        jp      x86_mem_write8

; IN: HL=16-bit physical address. OUT: A=value, carry on memory error.
bios_read_byte:
        ld      (x86_mem_addr), hl
        xor     a
        ld      (x86_mem_addr+2), a
        jp      x86_mem_read8

; IN: HL=address, A=expected byte. Carry if read fails or value differs.
bios_expect_byte:
        ld      (bios_work_value), a
        call    bios_read_byte
        ret     c
        ld      b, a
        ld      a, (bios_work_value)
        cp      b
        ret     z
        scf
        ret

bios_post_fail_1:
        ld      a, 1
        jr      bios_post_fail
bios_post_fail_2:
        ld      a, 2
        jr      bios_post_fail
bios_post_fail_3:
        ld      a, 3
        jr      bios_post_fail
bios_post_fail_4:
        ld      a, 4
        jr      bios_post_fail
bios_post_fail_5:
        ld      a, 5
        jr      bios_post_fail
bios_post_fail_6:
        ld      a, 6
        jr      bios_post_fail
bios_post_fail_7:
        ld      a, 7
        jr      bios_post_fail
bios_post_fail_8:
        ld      a, 8

bios_post_fail:
        ld      l, a
        ld      h, #B1
        ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
        ld      a, 7
        ld      (x86_exit_status), a
        scf
        ret

bios_clear_address:   dw 0
bios_clear_remaining: dw 0
bios_verify_address:  dw 0
bios_verify_remaining:dw 0
bios_work_value:      db 0

        ASSERT BIOS_IVT_BASE + BIOS_IVT_SIZE = BIOS_BDA_BASE
        ASSERT BIOS_BDA_BASE + BIOS_BDA_SIZE = BIOS_LOW_MEMORY_END


; =========================================================================
; FILE: neogs_sub/bios/bios_video.asm
; =========================================================================
; =========================================================================
; PROJECT 124 — BIOS INT 10h text services, Stage 3A
;
; Project text-console profile: 42 columns x 12 rows, 6x8 glyphs.
; Implemented functions:
;   AH=00h set mode 03h (project 42x12 text profile)
;   AH=0Fh get mode (AL=03h, AH=42, BH=0)
;   AH=02h set cursor (BH=0, DH=row, DL=column)
;   AH=03h get cursor (DH=row, DL=column)
;   AH=0Eh teletype output (AL=character, BH=0)
;
; INT 10h enters through the normal IVT/stack frame. The BIOS service itself
; is a firmware trap, then returns through the normal IRET primitive.
; Host stream protocol over DBIT/GSDAT:
;   nonzero byte = teletype character
;   00 00        = clear the lower debug console
;   00 01 row col= set lower debug console cursor
; =========================================================================

BIOS_VIDEO_COLUMNS EQU 42
BIOS_VIDEO_ROWS    EQU 12
BIOS_VIDEO_MODE    EQU #03

bios_int10_dispatch:
        ld      hl, (x86_reg_ax)
        ld      a, h
        cp      #00
        jp      z, bios_int10_set_mode
        cp      #0F
        jp      z, bios_int10_get_mode
        cp      #02
        jp      z, bios_int10_set_cursor
        cp      #03
        jp      z, bios_int10_get_cursor
        cp      #0E
        jp      z, bios_int10_teletype
        or      a
        ret

; Install IVT[10h] -> 0000:5200 (firmware service trap).
bios_int10_install_vector:
        ld      hl, 0
        ld      (x86_mem_segment), hl
        ld      hl, #0040
        ld      (x86_mem_offset), hl
        ld      hl, #5200
        ld      (x86_mem_word_value), hl
        call    x86_mem_write16
        ret     c
        ld      hl, 0
        ld      (x86_mem_segment), hl
        ld      hl, #0042
        ld      (x86_mem_offset), hl
        ld      hl, 0
        ld      (x86_mem_word_value), hl
        jp      x86_mem_write16

; AH=00h — only mode 03h is supported by this project console.
bios_int10_set_mode:
        ld      a, l
        cp      BIOS_VIDEO_MODE
        jr      z, .mode_valid
        or      a
        ret
.mode_valid:
        ld      hl, #0449
        ld      a, BIOS_VIDEO_MODE
        call    bios_video_write_byte
        ret     c
        ld      hl, #044A
        ld      a, BIOS_VIDEO_COLUMNS
        call    bios_video_write_byte
        ret     c
        ld      hl, #044C
        ld      a, #F0
        call    bios_video_write_byte
        ret     c
        ld      hl, #044D
        ld      a, #03
        call    bios_video_write_byte
        ret     c
        ld      hl, #0462
        xor     a
        call    bios_video_write_byte
        ret     c
        ld      hl, #0484
        ld      a, BIOS_VIDEO_ROWS - 1
        call    bios_video_write_byte
        ret     c
        ld      hl, #0485
        ld      a, 8
        call    bios_video_write_byte
        ret     c

        xor     a
        ld      (bios_video_col), a
        ld      (bios_video_row), a
        ld      hl, #0450
        ld      de, 0
        call    bios_video_write_word
        ret     c

        ; Host control frame: 00 00 = clear the 6x8 debug console.
        xor     a
        call    neo_send_data_byte
        ret     c
        xor     a
        jp      neo_send_data_byte

; AH=0Fh — return current project text mode, width and active page.
bios_int10_get_mode:
        ld      hl, #2A03
        ld      (x86_reg_ax), hl
        xor     a
        ld      (x86_reg_bx+1), a
        or      a
        ret

; AH=02h — set cursor. This implementation exposes page zero only.
bios_int10_set_cursor:
        ld      a, (x86_reg_bx+1)
        or      a
        ret     nz
        ld      hl, (x86_reg_dx)       ; H=DH row, L=DL column
        ld      a, h
        cp      BIOS_VIDEO_ROWS
        jr      c, .row_valid
        or      a
        ret
.row_valid:
        ld      a, l
        cp      BIOS_VIDEO_COLUMNS
        jr      c, .col_valid
        or      a
        ret
.col_valid:
        ld      a, h
        ld      (bios_video_row), a
        ld      a, l
        ld      (bios_video_col), a
        call    bios_video_store_cursor
        ret     c

        ; Host control frame: 00 01 row col.
        xor     a
        call    neo_send_data_byte
        ret     c
        ld      a, 1
        call    neo_send_data_byte
        ret     c
        ld      a, (bios_video_row)
        call    neo_send_data_byte
        ret     c
        ld      a, (bios_video_col)
        jp      neo_send_data_byte

; AH=03h — read page-zero cursor into DH:DL.
bios_int10_get_cursor:
        ld      a, (x86_reg_bx+1)
        or      a
        ret     nz
        ld      hl, #0450
        ld      (x86_mem_offset), hl
        ld      hl, 0
        ld      (x86_mem_segment), hl
        call    x86_mem_read16
        ret     c
        ld      (x86_reg_dx), hl
        or      a
        ret

; AH=0Eh — teletype output. Every byte is streamed to the host.
bios_int10_teletype:
        ld      a, (x86_reg_bx+1)
        or      a
        ret     nz
        ld      a, (x86_reg_ax)
        ld      (bios_video_char), a
        or      a
        ret     z                       ; NUL is reserved for stream control frames.
        cp      128
        jr      c, .teletype_ascii
        ld      a, '?'
        ld      (bios_video_char), a
.teletype_ascii:
        ld      a, (bios_video_char)
        call    neo_send_data_byte
        ret     c

        ld      a, (bios_video_char)
        cp      13
        jr      z, .carriage_return
        cp      10
        jr      z, .line_feed
        cp      8
        jr      z, .backspace
        cp      7
        jr      z, .cursor_done
        cp      32
        jr      c, .cursor_done

        ld      a, (bios_video_col)
        inc     a
        ld      (bios_video_col), a
        cp      BIOS_VIDEO_COLUMNS
        jr      c, .cursor_store
        xor     a
        ld      (bios_video_col), a
        jr      .line_feed
.carriage_return:
        xor     a
        ld      (bios_video_col), a
        jr      .cursor_store
.backspace:
        ld      a, (bios_video_col)
        or      a
        jr      z, .cursor_done
        dec     a
        ld      (bios_video_col), a
        jr      .cursor_store
.line_feed:
        ld      a, (bios_video_row)
        inc     a
        cp      BIOS_VIDEO_ROWS
        jr      c, .line_store
        ld      a, BIOS_VIDEO_ROWS - 1
.line_store:
        ld      (bios_video_row), a
.cursor_store:
        call    bios_video_store_cursor
        ret
.cursor_done:
        or      a
        ret

; Store project cursor word at BDA 00450h: low=column, high=row.
bios_video_store_cursor:
        ld      hl, #0450
        ld      (x86_mem_offset), hl
        ld      hl, 0
        ld      (x86_mem_segment), hl
        ld      a, (bios_video_col)
        ld      l, a
        ld      a, (bios_video_row)
        ld      h, a
        ld      (x86_mem_word_value), hl
        jp      x86_mem_write16

; IN: HL=physical address, A=value.
bios_video_write_byte:
        ld      (bios_video_write_value), a
        ld      (x86_mem_addr), hl
        xor     a
        ld      (x86_mem_addr+2), a
        ld      a, (bios_video_write_value)
        ld      (x86_mem_value), a
        jp      x86_mem_write8

; IN: HL=physical address, DE=value.
bios_video_write_word:
        ld      (x86_mem_offset), hl
        ld      hl, 0
        ld      (x86_mem_segment), hl
        ex      de, hl
        ld      (x86_mem_word_value), hl
        jp      x86_mem_write16

; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
; Stage 3A executable test. Exercises all five INT 10h services, cursor
; round-trip, 42-column wrap, CR/LF, and the streamed host console path.
; Expected final AX=1A10h, DX=020Fh, BDA cursor=020Fh.
; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
bios_int10_video_test:
        xor     a
        ld      (x86_real_bounds_enable), a
        call    x86_mem_init
        ld      hl, x86_bios_video_test_image
        ld      de, #5002
        ld      bc, x86_bios_video_test_image_end - x86_bios_video_test_image
        call    x86_real_copy_image
        jp      c, bios_video_test_fail_1

        ; Verify POST installed IVT[10h] -> 0000:5200.
        ld      hl, 0
        ld      (x86_mem_segment), hl
        ld      hl, #0040
        ld      (x86_mem_offset), hl
        call    x86_mem_read16
        jp      c, bios_video_test_fail_2
        ld      de, #5200
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      nz, bios_video_test_fail_2

        ld      hl, 0
        ld      (x86_mem_segment), hl
        ld      hl, #0042
        ld      (x86_mem_offset), hl
        call    x86_mem_read16
        jp      c, bios_video_test_fail_2
        ld      de, 0
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      nz, bios_video_test_fail_2

        ; Initialize guest registers and real-mode stack.
        ld      hl, 0
        ld      (x86_reg_ax), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_bx), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_cx), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_dx), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_bp), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_si), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_di), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_es), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_ds), hl
        ld      (x86_reg_cs), hl
        ld      hl, #5002
        ld      (x86_reg_ip), hl
        ld      hl, #7000
        ld      (x86_reg_ss), hl
        ld      hl, #0100
        ld      (x86_reg_sp), hl
        ld      hl, #0202
        ld      (x86_reg_flags), hl

        call    x86_real_execute
        jp      c, bios_video_test_fail_3

        ld      hl, (x86_reg_ax)
        ld      de, #2A03
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      nz, bios_video_test_fail_4
        ld      hl, (x86_reg_dx)
        ld      de, #020F
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      nz, bios_video_test_fail_5

        ld      hl, #0450
        ld      (x86_mem_offset), hl
        ld      hl, 0
        ld      (x86_mem_segment), hl
        call    x86_mem_read16
        jp      c, bios_video_test_fail_6
        ld      de, #020F
        or      a
        sbc     hl, de
        jp      nz, bios_video_test_fail_6

        ld      hl, #1A10
        ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
        xor     a
        ld      (x86_exit_status), a
        or      a
        ret

bios_video_test_fail_1:
        ld      a, 1
        jr      bios_video_test_fail
bios_video_test_fail_2:
        ld      a, 2
        jr      bios_video_test_fail
bios_video_test_fail_3:
        ld      a, 3
        jr      bios_video_test_fail
bios_video_test_fail_4:
        ld      a, 4
        jr      bios_video_test_fail
bios_video_test_fail_5:
        ld      a, 5
        jr      bios_video_test_fail
bios_video_test_fail_6:
        ld      a, 6
        jr      bios_video_test_fail
bios_video_test_fail_7:
        ld      a, 7
bios_video_test_fail:
        ld      l, a
        ld      h, #1C
        ld      (saved_x86_ax), hl
        ld      a, 10
        ld      (x86_exit_status), a
        scf
        ret

bios_video_row: db 0
bios_video_col: db 0
bios_video_char: db 0
bios_video_write_value: db 0

; Guest program is assembled as a byte stream at physical 0000:5002.
x86_bios_video_test_image:
        ; AH=00h set mode 03h.
        db #B8,#03,#00,#CD,#10
        ; AH=02h set cursor to row 1, column 5; teletype X there.
        db #B8,#00,#02,#BB,#00,#00,#BA,#05,#01,#CD,#10
        db #B4,#0E,#B0,#58,#CD,#10 ; X — visible cursor-position marker
        ; AH=02h reset cursor to row 0, column 0 before the wrap test.
        db #B8,#00,#02,#BB,#00,#00,#BA,#00,#00,#CD,#10
        ; 43 printable characters: the 43rd must wrap to row 1, column 1.
        REPT 43
        db #B4,#0E,#B0,#41,#CD,#10
        ENDR
        ; CR then LF; output a 15-character marker on row 2.
        db #B4,#0E,#B0,#0D,#CD,#10
        db #B4,#0E,#B0,#0A,#CD,#10
        db #B4,#0E,#B0,#50,#CD,#10 ; P
        db #B4,#0E,#B0,#31,#CD,#10 ; 1
        db #B4,#0E,#B0,#32,#CD,#10 ; 2
        db #B4,#0E,#B0,#34,#CD,#10 ; 4
        db #B4,#0E,#B0,#20,#CD,#10 ; space
        db #B4,#0E,#B0,#49,#CD,#10 ; I
        db #B4,#0E,#B0,#4E,#CD,#10 ; N
        db #B4,#0E,#B0,#54,#CD,#10 ; T
        db #B4,#0E,#B0,#31,#CD,#10 ; 1
        db #B4,#0E,#B0,#30,#CD,#10 ; 0
        db #B4,#0E,#B0,#20,#CD,#10 ; space
        db #B4,#0E,#B0,#50,#CD,#10 ; P
        db #B4,#0E,#B0,#41,#CD,#10 ; A
        db #B4,#0E,#B0,#53,#CD,#10 ; S
        db #B4,#0E,#B0,#53,#CD,#10 ; S
        ; AH=03h get cursor, then AH=0Fh get mode/columns.
        db #B4,#03,#B0,#00,#BB,#00,#00,#CD,#10
        db #B4,#0F,#B0,#00,#CD,#10
        db #F4
x86_bios_video_test_image_end:


; =========================================================================
; FILE: neogs_sub/build_firmware.asm
; =========================================================================
; =========================================================================
; Build NeoGS firmware and one deployable package from the Project 124 root:
;   sjasmplus neogs_sub/build_firmware.asm
;
; neogs_code.bin       custom firmware, loaded at logical RAM address $0000
; neogs_bootstrap.bin  intermediate bootstrap; neoprog.bin packages it with firmware
; =========================================================================
        DEVICE ZXSPECTRUM128
        INCLUDE "main.asm"

; Resident firmware occupies WIN0. WIN2 is being reserved for banked
; firmware overlays; the resident Z80 stack is in WIN1/page #03 at $4FFE.
; WIN3 remains the independently switched x86 physical-memory window.
        ASSERT neogs_firmware_end <= $4000
        SAVEBIN "neogs_code.bin", 0, neogs_firmware_end
        SAVEBIN "zx_main/neogs_code.bin", 0, neogs_firmware_end

; GS105a #14 writes the executable bootstrap into fixed RAM $4000-$7FFF.
        ORG     $4000
        INCLUDE "bootstrap.asm"
        ASSERT neogs_bootstrap_start = $4000
        ASSERT neogs_bootstrap_end <= $7FFF
; The host keeps the first TR-DOS sector in its reusable #C000 buffer while
; it installs and starts this bootstrap. Header + bootstrap must fit in 256 bytes.
        ASSERT neogs_bootstrap_end - neogs_bootstrap_start <= 254
        SAVEBIN "neogs_bootstrap.bin", neogs_bootstrap_start, neogs_bootstrap_end - neogs_bootstrap_start
        SAVEBIN "zx_main/neogs_bootstrap.bin", neogs_bootstrap_start, neogs_bootstrap_end - neogs_bootstrap_start

; neoprog.bin is assembled by the build scripts after both intermediate
; binaries have been written. It is the only NeoGS payload exported to TR-DOS.
